走向肝细胞癌的精准治疗：风险分层和治疗指导的临床推理法学硕士Towards Precision Therapy in Hepatocellular Carcinoma: A Clinical-Reasoning LLM for Risk Stratification and Treatment Guidance

摘要Abstract

肝细胞癌（HCC）是全球最常见的恶性肿瘤之一，并且仍然是癌症相关死亡的主要原因。然而，现行指南和分期系统仅描绘了粗略的类别，未能捕捉阶段内的异质性，并忽视了电子病历 (EMR) 中嵌入的临床背景。为了弥补这一差距，我们提出Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) ranks among the most prevalent malignancies worldwide and remains a leading cause of cancer-related mortality. However, prevailing guidelines and staging systems delineate only coarse categories, failing to capture within-stage heterogeneity and overlooking the clinical context embedded in electronic medical records (EMRs). To bridge this gap, we presentHCC-STAR（肝细胞癌分期、治疗和预后），一种临床对齐的大语言模型，可读取常规 EMR 叙述并联合输出基于风险评分的分期、具有循证原理的指南一致治疗的排名列表以及个体化生存估计。我们从 SEER 中整理了大约 30,000 个 HCC 病例，并通过临床医生验证的、基于提示的增强工作流程将它们扩展为 EMR 式叙述训练数据。在此语料库的基础上，我们提出了一个以知识为中心、以推理为中心的训练框架，在可逐步验证的复合奖励下进行优化，该框架不同于临床指南的文本级记忆。通过对来自中国 12 家医院的 6,668 名患者的多中心队列进行评估，与临床指南和竞争模型（特别是 GPT-5 和 Gemini-2.5 Pro）相比，HCC-STAR 在治疗推荐和风险分层方面取得了最先进的表现。假设的总体生存分析表明，遵守模型建议的中位生存期为 51 个月，而 BCLC 和 CNLC 的中位生存期分别为 29 个月和 32 个月。在以临床医生为中心的评估中，盲法肝胆专家将 HCC-STAR 的思路和基于证据的理由评为值得信赖。该模型在治疗准确性方面超越了住院医生和主治医生，当用作助手时，可以帮助他们更快地做出更准确的决策。这些发现支持 HCC-STAR 作为 HCC 风险分层和精准治疗的可靠且可验证的决策支持系统。(Hepatocellular Carcinoma Staging, Treatment And pRognosis), a clinically aligned large language model that reads routine EMR narratives and jointly outputs a risk score-based staging, a ranked list of guideline-consistent treatments with evidence-based rationales, and individualized survival estimates. We curate approximately 30,000 HCC cases from SEER and expand them into EMR-style narrative training data via a clinician-validated, prompt-based augmentation workflow. Building on this corpus, we propose a knowledge-aligned, reasoning-centric training framework, optimized under a step-verifiable composite reward, that departs from text-level memorization of clinical guidelines. Evaluated on the multi-center cohort of 6,668 patients from 12 hospitals across China, HCC-STAR achieves state-of-the-art performance in treatment recommendation and risk stratification compared with clinical guidelines and competitive models, particularly GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5 Pro. Hypothetical overall-survival analyses indicate that the median survival under adherence to the model’s recommendations was 51 months, compared with 29 and 32 months under BCLC and CNLC, respectively. In a clinician-centric evaluation, blinded hepatobiliary specialists rate HCC-STAR’s chain of thought and evidence-based justifications as trustworthy. The model surpasses resident and attending physicians in treatment accuracy and, when used as an assistant, helps them make more accurate decisions faster. These findings support HCC-STAR as a reliable and verifiable decision-support system for risk stratification and precision therapy in HCC.

关键词：Keywords:肝细胞癌；大语言模型；强化学习；精准治疗；生存预测Hepatocellular carcinoma; large language model; reinforcement learning; precision therapy; survival prognosis

1 主要1 Main

肝细胞癌 (HCC) 是全球第六大常见恶性肿瘤，也是癌症相关死亡的第三大原因，在东亚和撒哈拉以南非洲地区发病率尤其高[1, 2]。尽管在筛查、手术技术和全身治疗方面取得了进步，但由于治疗延迟或次优以及肿瘤生物学和患者状况固有的异质性，总体结果仍然很差[3, 4]。对大型现实世界队列的分析表明，很大一部分患者在诊断后经历了超过 90 天的治疗延迟，这与生存率降低密切相关，特别是在疾病早期 [5, 6]。 HCC 的临床决策本质上是复杂的：临床医生必须通过多种方式综合有关肿瘤负荷、肝功能、体能状态、血管侵犯和肝外扩散的信息。在实践中，此类决策往往取决于少数资深肝胆专家以及在有限的时间内反复进行的多学科讨论。获得专家咨询的机会有限，医疗资源不均衡，导致错失及时、个性化治疗的机会，导致现实世界的结果不理想。Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is the sixth most common malignancy worldwide and the third leading cause of cancer-related mortality, with particularly high incidence in East Asia and sub-Saharan Africa [1, 2]. Despite advances in screening, surgical technique, and systemic therapy, overall outcomes remain poor owing to delayed or suboptimal treatment and the inherent heterogeneity of tumor biology and patient conditions [3, 4]. Analyses of large real-world cohorts indicate that a substantial proportion of patients experience treatment delays exceeding 90 days from diagnosis, which are strongly associated with reduced survival, particularly in early-stage disease [5, 6]. Clinical decision-making in HCC is inherently complex: clinicians must synthesize information on tumor burden, liver function, performance status, vascular invasion, and extrahepatic spread across multiple modalities. In practice, such decisions frequently depend on a small number of senior hepatobiliary specialists and iterative multidisciplinary discussions within constrained time frames. Limited access to expert consultation and uneven healthcare resources contribute to missed opportunities for timely, individualized therapy, resulting in suboptimal real-world outcomes.

在肝病学数十年的进步过程中，共识指南和分期系统，包括 AJCC (TNM) [7]、BCLC [8] 和 CNLC [9]，通过将分期系统与首选治疗途径联系起来，建立了治疗选择的结构化原则。然而，这些框架主要是为人群水平的管理而设计的，不足以适应患者水平的异质性，例如边缘肝储备、多灶性疾病、合并症或细微的影像表型[10]。因此，他们缺乏在单一工作流程中将个性化治疗建议与预后估计相结合的灵活性。此外，掌握指南的复杂性和积累基于案例的专业知识需要广泛的培训和经验，这阻碍了在不同的医疗保健环境中做出一致的、高质量的决策。在乙肝病毒为主的队列中，精细的风险分层进一步描绘了从治疗或强化干预措施中获得更大益处的亚组，强调了单独基于指南的分期在实现个性化护理方面的局限性[11]。总的来说，这些挑战凸显了对基于临床的人工智能的需求，它将细粒度的临床变量与个体患者层面的风险分层和治疗决策相结合。Over the course of decades of progress in hepatology, consensus guidelines and staging systems, including AJCC (TNM) [7], BCLC [8], and CNLC [9], have established structured principles for treatment selection by linking staging systems to preferred treatment pathways. Nevertheless, these frameworks were primarily designed for population-level management and insufficiently accommodate patient-level heterogeneity, such as marginal hepatic reserve, multifocal disease, comorbidities, or nuanced imaging phenotypes [10]. As a result, they lack the flexibility to combine individualized treatment recommendations with prognostic estimation within a single workflow. Moreover, mastering guideline intricacies and accumulating case-based expertise require extensive training and experience, hindering consistent, high-quality decision-making across diverse healthcare settings. In HBV-predominant cohorts, refined risk stratification further delineates subgroups that derive greater benefit from curative or intensified interventions, underscoring the limitations of guideline-based staging alone in achieving personalized care [11]. Collectively, these challenges highlight the need for clinically grounded artificial intelligence that integrates fine-grained clinical variables with risk stratification and treatment decision-making at the level of individual patients.

大语言模型 (LLM) [12, 13] 和推理增强训练范例 [14, 15, 16] 的最新进展为临床决策支持提供了有希望的途径。然而，大多数现有的工作和卓越的表现仍然以诊断为中心[17,18,19,20,21]，并通过明确定义的标准和策划的数据集来促进，而相对较少的解决Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) [12, 13] and reasoning-enhanced training paradigms [14, 15, 16] offer promising avenues for clinical decision support. However, most existing works and remarkable performance remain diagnosis-centric [17, 18, 19, 20, 21], facilitated by well-defined criteria and curated datasets, whereas comparatively few address治疗建议treatment recommendation or 纵向决策longitudinal decision-making在肿瘤学中。诊断基准的高分并不能保证对异质现实世界队列的治疗建议或风险分层的稳健概括，因为治疗计划是一个多步骤的过程，必须整合不同的临床数据并不断更新。有限的跨机构验证和缺乏肿瘤特异性适应性进一步限制了临床可靠性[22]。最近的研究 [23, 24] 还表明，即使最先进的法学硕士在诊断任务和医疗许可考试（例如 PubMedQA [25]、USMLE [26]）上实现了高精度，他们在治疗计划方面的表现仍然要低得多，并且与指南一致的建议在现实的临床工作流程中通常不可靠。除了诊断之外，一些研究已经开始探索疾病管理和纵向护理，利用法学硕士和多模态视觉语言系统进行原发性糖尿病管理和视网膜疾病筛查[27, 28]。对于 HCC，虽然先前的工作 [29] 试图通过以下方式优化治疗（消融和切除）：in oncology. High scores on diagnostic benchmarks can not guarantee robust generalization to heterogeneous real-world cohorts for treatment recommendations or risk stratifications because treatment planning is a multi-step process that must integrate diverse clinical data and be continually updated. Limited cross-institutional validation and the absence of oncology-specific adaptation further constrain clinical reliability [22]. Recent studies [23, 24] also show that, even when state-of-the-art LLMs achieve high accuracy on diagnostic tasks and medical licensing exams (e.g., PubMedQA [25], USMLE [26]), their performance on treatment planning remains substantially lower, and guideline-concordant recommendations are often unreliable in realistic clinical workflows. Beyond diagnosis, a few studies have begun to explore disease management and longitudinal care by leveraging LLMs and multimodal vision–language systems for primary diabetes management and retinal disease screening [27, 28]. For HCC, while prior work [29] attempts to optimize treatment (ablation and resection) with a

2

Vision Transformer [30]用于满足米兰标准的复发性 HCC，其仅成像设计和有限的决策空间达不到本文更广泛的目标，即与指南一致的治疗建议、细粒度分期指导和生存预测。Vision Transformer [30] for recurrent HCC meeting Milan criteria, its imaging-only design and limited decision space fall short of the broader aims of this paper, i.e., guideline-aligned treatment recommendation, fine-grained staging guidance, and survival prediction.

为了解决这些限制，我们引入To address these limitations, we introduceHCC-STAR（肝细胞癌分期、治疗和预后），一个领域适应模型，将细粒度 HCC 分期、个体化治疗建议和生存预测集成在统一框架内（图 1c）。对于模型开发，我们首先整理了大约 30,000 个 HCC 病例，并使用来自监测、流行病学和最终结果 (SEER) 计划 [31] 的预后注释作为主要训练语料库。然而，SEER 仅包含一组有限的结构化变量，例如年龄、肿瘤大小、甲胎蛋白 (AFP)、TNM 分期和生存月数，但缺乏法学硕士监督微调和推理导向学习所需的丰富文本细节。为了弥补这一差距，我们设计了一个基于提示的数据增强管道，该管道以提炼的临床知识为基础，包括治疗指南和基于证据的理由，这些知识源自中国原发性肝癌转化和围手术期治疗专家共识（CNLC 2024）[32]和去识别化的真实世界电子病历（EMR）（图1a）。通过这个过程，法学硕士将结构化条目从 SEER 扩展到连贯的 EMR 风格的叙述，从而产生大规模且临床现实的语料库。该策略弥补了缺失的临床背景并提高了合成数据的可靠性，为模型训练奠定了坚实的基础。(Hepatocellular Carcinoma Staging, Treatment And pRognosis), a domain-adapted model that integrates fine-grained HCC staging, individualized treatment recommendation, and survival prediction within a unified framework (Fig. 1c). For model development, we first curate approximately 30,000 HCC cases with prognostic annotations from Surveillance, Epidemiology, and End Results (SEER) program [31] as the primary training corpus. However, SEER contains only a limited set of structured variables, such as age, tumor size, alpha-fetoprotein (AFP), TNM stage, and survival months, but lacks the rich textual detail required for supervised fine-tuning and reasoning-oriented learning in LLMs. To bridge this gap, we design a prompt-based data augmentation pipeline informed by distilled clinical knowledge, including treatment guidelines and evidence-based justifications, derived from both the Chinese Expert Consensus on Conversion and Perioperative Therapy of Primary Liver Cancer (CNLC 2024) [32] and de-identified real-world electronic medical records (EMRs) (Fig. 1a). Through this process, LLMs expand structured entries from SEER into coherent EMR-style narratives, yielding a large-scale and clinically realistic corpus. This strategy compensates for missing clinical context and improves the reliability of synthetic data, establishing a robust foundation for model training.

我们采用两阶段训练范式，实施We adopt a two-stage training paradigm that implements a知识对齐推理范式knowledge-aligned reasoning paradigm而不是文本级指南记忆（图 1b）。在第一阶段，rather than text-level guideline memorization (Fig. 1b). In Stage 1,临床知识熟悉微调 (CKF-FT)Clinical-Knowledge Familiarization Fine-Tuning (CKF-FT)通过以下步骤指导模型充当肝胆临床医生guides the model to behave as a hepatobiliary clinician by working through临床实例worked clinical examples使用 EMR 风格的叙述，并辅以指南逻辑和证据引用。通过这种方式，通过实践而不是逐字复制来学习符合指南的推理和基于临床的输出。在第二阶段，using EMR-style narratives augmented with guideline logic and evidence citations. In this way, guideline-consistent reasoning and clinically grounded outputs are learned through practice rather than verbatim copying. In Stage 2,经验累积强化学习（EARL）Experience-Accrual Reinforcement Learning (EARL)通过两个中心设计选择，在临床定制的复合奖励下完善临床推理。首先，奖励是可逐步验证的：它检查思想链（CoT）中的中间变量，包括分期线索、表现状态、Child-Pugh 等级、血管侵犯和证据引用，而不是只对最终答案进行评分，以便模型因正确原因得出正确答案而获得奖励。其次，在组相对策略优化（GRPO）[16]期间，治疗排名和生存估计奖励是解耦的，防止排名监督和审查时间监督之间的梯度干扰——否则这两个目标会将政策拉向不同的方向。这些选择共同将指导逻辑转换为可审核的、多目标的和稳定的学习信号。鉴于指南逻辑是在推理过程中练习而不是记忆，增量指南更新可以通过系统提示编辑来合并，而无需模型重新训练。在这两个阶段，该框架端到端地吸收异质的临床叙述（诊断记录、影像报告、手术总结、出院文件），鼓励模型揭示显着因素并将推理步骤与符合指南的行动联系起来。多目标推理框架产生准确、可解释且临床可信的 CoT [33]，使 HCC-STAR 能够遵守既定标准，同时迭代提高治疗精度和个体化风险分层。refines clinical reasoning under a clinically tailored composite reward with two central design choices. First, the reward is step-verifiable: it inspects intermediate variables in the chain of thought (CoT), including staging cues, performance status, Child–Pugh grade, vascular invasion and evidence citations, rather than scoring only the final answer, so that the model is rewarded for arriving at the right answer for the right reason. Second, treatment-ranking and survival-estimation rewards are decoupled during Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) [16], preventing gradient interference between ranking supervision and censored-time supervision—two objectives that otherwise pull the policy in different directions. Together, these choices convert guideline logic into an auditable, multi-objective and stable learning signal. Given that guideline logic is exercised during reasoning rather than memorized, incremental guideline updates can be incorporated via system-prompt edits without model retraining. Across both stages, the framework ingests heterogeneous clinical narratives end-to-end (diagnostic notes, imaging reports, operative summaries, discharge documentation), encouraging the model to surface salient factors and link reasoning steps to guideline-aligned actions. The multi-objective reasoning framework yields the CoT [33] that is accurate, interpretable, and clinically faithful, enabling HCC-STAR to adhere to established standards while iteratively improving treatment precision and individualized risk stratification.

在回顾性队列和盲法临床评估中，HCC-STAR 在治疗准确性和生存率方面始终优于传统机器学习基线、代表性大语言模型（GPT-5 [34]、Gemini-2.5-Pro [35]、GPT-4o [36]、Claude [37]、DeepSeek-R1 [38]）和主要基于指南的分期系统（AJCC/TNM、BCLC、CNLC）预测。在假设的总体生存分析中，遵守模型的建议与 BCLC/CNLC 相比，生存期显着延长（中位 OS 51 个月 vs 29-32 个月），凸显了潜在的临床实用性。与大多数在固定和结构化模式上训练的 ML 模型（通常无法跨异构数据格式进行泛化）不同，我们的方法可以解释 EMR 风格的叙述，捕获非线性临床交互，并生成明确的 CoT 基本原理，从而无需重新训练即可实现跨机构泛化。在外部多中心验证中，该模型实现了最高的 C 指数和 AUROC，证明了捕获阶段内异质性的连续风险估计的好处。在医师水平比较中，HCC-STAR在治疗准确性方面超越初级和中级临床医生，并接近高级专家的表现。当用作辅助工具时，它可以显着提高经验不足的医生的准确性和效率，缩小决策质量的差异。在肝胆专家主导的结构化评估中，我们的模型的 CoT 推理被认为比 GPT-4o 和 DeepSeek-R1 更完整、更准确、更安全，并且具有更精确、更可靠的循证论证。总而言之，据我们所知，这些结果使 HCC-STAR 成为第一个将 HCC 风险分层和治疗决策统一起来的领域适应推理模型，强调了其在推进肝病学领域法学硕士辅助临床决策方面的潜力，并促进以推理为中心的法学硕士在疾病治疗和预测方面的更广泛探索。Across retrospective cohorts and blinded clinical evaluations, HCC-STAR consistently outperforms traditional machinelearning baselines, representative large language models (GPT-5 [34], Gemini-2.5-Pro [35], GPT-4o [36], Claude [37], DeepSeek-R1 [38]), and major guideline-based staging systems (AJCC/TNM, BCLC, CNLC) for both treatment accuracy and survival prediction. In hypothetical overall-survival analyses, adherence to the model’s recommendations is associated with substantially longer survival than following BCLC/CNLC (median OS 51 vs 29–32 months), highlighting potential clinical utility. Unlike most ML models trained on fixed and structured schemas that often fail to generalize across heterogeneous data formats, our approach interprets EMR-style narratives, captures nonlinear clinical interactions, and generates explicit CoT rationales, enabling cross-institutional generalization without retraining. In external multi-center validation, the model achieves the highest C-index and AUROC, demonstrating the benefit of continuous risk estimation that captures within-stage heterogeneity. In physician-level comparisons, HCC-STAR surpasses junior and intermediate clinicians in treatment accuracy and approaches the performance of senior specialists. When used as an assistive tool, it significantly improves the accuracy and efficiency of less experienced physicians, narrowing disparities in decision quality. In a structured evaluation led by hepatobiliary experts, our model’s CoT reasoning is rated more complete, accurate, and safer than that of GPT-4o and DeepSeek-R1, and with more precise and reliable evidence-based justification. Taken together, these results position HCC-STAR as, to our knowledge, the first domain-adapted reasoning model that unifies risk stratification and treatment decision-making in HCC, underscoring its potential to advance LLM-assisted clinical decision-making in hepatology and catalyze broader exploration of reasoning-centric LLMs in disease therapy and prognostication.

2 个结果2 Results

2.1 数据特征和模型开发2.1 Data characteristics and model development

我们首先对用于模型开发和评估的内部 SEER 队列和外部多中心 HCC 队列进行了表征。 SEER 计划为内部提供了约 30,000 个结构化 HCC 病例（2004 年至 2020 年诊断）We first characterized the internal SEER cohort and the external multicenter HCC cohort used for model development and evaluation. The SEER Program contributed ∼30,000 structured HCC cases (diagnosed 2004–2020) for internal

3

∼ ∼ 训练（28,000）和测试（2,000）。与此同时，我们收集了来自中国 12 家三级医院的 6,668 名肝细胞癌患者的真实外部队列进行外部验证。该人群以男性为主（5,420/6,668；81.3%），中心年龄中位数集中在 50 多岁（中心中位数的中位数 ≈ 59 岁；范围为 52-61 岁）。对于生存分析，我们通过排除围手术期死亡、缺失随访和不完整分期，得出了生存过滤测试集，产生了 4,190 名具有相似基线特征的患者。多中心 EMR 记录常规肝病学实践，包括 ECOG 体能状态 (PS)、Child-Pugh 评分、实验室指标、肿瘤标志物、影像学结果和活检病理学（如果有）。训练数据包括用于下游模型训练的结构化表格和临床叙述（图 1a）。在 140 个合成 EMR 中，三名高级肝胆临床医生在六个预先指定的维度上完成了盲法保真度评估，并允许不适用的领域进行 NA。在 2,518 个非 NA 维度级别评级中，总体保真度得分为 4.81 ± 0.55，中位数为 5（IQR，5-5）。临床可接受的评级（分数≥4）占所有评级的96.0%，而低保真评级（分数≤2）并不常见（1.2%）。在创纪录的水平上，137/140 份综合 EMR (97.9%) 的平均得分至少为 4。评估者间一致性很高，两两一分内一致性为 97.1%，三评估者一分内一致性为 95.2%，ICC(2,k) 为 0.73。这些发现支持了即时增强 EMR 叙述的临床合理性和内部一致性（扩展数据表 1）。∼ ∼ training ( 28,000) and testing ( 2,000). In parallel, we assembled a real-world external cohort of 6,668 patients with hepatocellular carcinoma from 12 tertiary hospitals across China for external validation. This cohort exhibits a male predominance (5,420/6,668; 81.3%) and center-level median ages clustered in the late 50s (median of center medians ≈ 59 years; range, 52–61 years). For survival analyses, we derived a survival-filtered test set by excluding perioperative deaths, missing follow-up, and incomplete staging, yielding 4,190 patients with similar baseline characteristics. The multi-center EMRs capture routine hepatology practice, including ECOG performance status (PS), Child–Pugh score, laboratory indices, tumor markers, imaging findings, and biopsy pathology when available. The training data comprise structured tables and clinical narratives for downstream model training (Fig. 1a). Across the 140 synthetic EMRs, three senior hepatobiliary clinicians completed blinded fidelity assessment across six pre-specified dimensions, with NA allowed for non-applicable fields. Across 2,518 non-NA dimension-level ratings, the overall fidelity score was 4.81 ± 0.55, with a median of 5 (IQR, 5–5). Clinically acceptable ratings (score ≥4) accounted for 96.0% of all ratings, whereas low-fidelity ratings (score ≤2) were uncommon (1.2%). At the record level, 137/140 synthetic EMRs (97.9%) achieved a mean score of at least 4. Inter-rater consistency was high, with pairwise within-one-point agreement of 97.1%, three-rater within-one-point agreement of 95.2%, and ICC(2,k) of 0.73. These findings support the clinical plausibility and internal consistency of the prompt-augmented EMR narratives (Extended Data Table 1).

基于这些数据，我们在两阶段知识对齐推理范式下训练了三个 LLM 系列（即 Qwen3 [39]、QWQ [40] 和 DeepSeek-R1 [38]）（图 1b）。Building on these data, we then trained three families of LLMs (i.e., Qwen3 [39], QWQ [40], and DeepSeek-R1 [38]) under a two-stage, knowledge-aligned reasoning paradigm (Fig. 1b).第一阶段：临床知识熟悉微调：Stage 1: Clinical-Knowledge Familiarization Fine-Tuning:该模型被提示充当肝胆临床医生，并从工作的临床实例（注入指南逻辑和证据的 EMR 风格的叙述）中学习，将输出与指南一致的推理和临床基础文本保持一致。the model is prompted to act as a hepatobiliary clinician and learn from worked clinical examples (EMR-style narratives infused with guideline logic and evidence), aligning outputs with guideline-consistent reasoning and clinically grounded text.第二阶段：经验累积强化学习：Stage 2: Experience-Accrual Reinforcement Learning:使用强化学习和临床定制的、可验证的复合奖励进一步完善推理能力和决策质量，共同优化正确性、输出格式有效性、治疗排名一致性、生存估计、简洁性和 CoT 质量。该系统产生明确的中间标签（例如，PS和Child-Pugh评分）并返回基于风险评分的分期、具有基于证据的基本原理的推荐治疗的排名列表以及患者特定的生存估计（图1c）。reasoning capacity and decision quality are further refined using reinforcement learning with a clinically tailored, verifiable composite reward that jointly optimizes correctness, output-format validity, treatment-ranking consistency, survival estimation, brevity, and CoT quality. The system produces explicit intermediate tags (for example, PS and Child–Pugh score) and returns a risk score-based staging, a ranked list of recommended therapies with evidence-based rationale, and patient-specific survival estimates (Fig. 1c).

2.2 治疗推荐绩效评价2.2 Evaluation of treatment recommendation performance

我们首先使用排名感知的顶级评估治疗建议的质量We first assessed the quality of treatment recommendations using a rank-aware Top-𝑘衡量列表重叠和排名一致性的准确性。在外部多中心队列和内部 SEER 测试集上，图 2a 和图 2b 显示 HCC-STAR 取得了最高的 Top-accuracy that measures both list overlap and rank concordance. Across the external multi-center cohort and the internal SEER test set, Fig. 2a and Fig. 2b show that HCC-STAR achieves the highest Top-𝑘准确度为accuracy for𝑘ε 1∈ 1, 2 ,3 与临床指南（BCLC、CNLC）和代表性法学硕士（GPT-5、Gemini-2.5-pro、GPT-4o、Claude）进行比较。特别是，我们的模型显着优于 GPT-5 和 Gemini-2.5-pro，验证了强化学习对 HCC 特定临床知识和基于病例的临床推理的有效性。在内部 SEER 集上，它也优于传统的机器学习基线（例如支持向量机、XGBoost）和其他方法。 Top-1 准确性的收益最为明显，这具有临床意义，因为一线选择通常决定治疗计划和下游预后。排名感知相似性同样表明与指南一致的参考列表有更大的重叠和更接近的排名一致性。3 compared with clinical guidelines (BCLC, CNLC) and representative LLMs (GPT-5, Gemini-2.5-pro, GPT-4o, Claude). Especially, our model significantly outperforms GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5-pro, which verifies the effectiveness of reinforcement learning on HCC-specific clinical knowledge and case-based clinical reasoning. On the internal SEER set, it also outperforms traditional machine-learning baselines (e.g., support vector machines, XGBoost) and other methods. Gains are most pronounced for Top-1 accuracy, which is clinically meaningful because the first-line option often determines the treatment plan and downstream prognosis. The rank-aware similarity likewise indicates greater overlap and closer rank agreement with the guideline-consistent reference lists.

然后，我们通过对 60 例精心策划的 HCC 病例进行两次以医生为中心的评估来检查临床可靠性，并在扩展数据图 1 中总结了患者统计数据（年龄、分期、ECOG PS、Child-Pugh）。在一项盲法专家评分研究中，三名高级肝胆专家独立审查并评分了来自我们的模型和两位领先法学硕士的匿名思想链和建议。 HCC-STAR 获得了最高的综合质量得分和最高的We then examined clinical reliability through two physician-centric evaluations on 60 carefully curated HCC cases, and patient statistics (age, staging, ECOG PS, Child–Pugh) are summarized in Extended Data Fig. 1. In a blinded expert scoring study, three senior hepatobiliary specialists independently reviewed and scored anonymized chains of thought and recommendations from our model and two leading LLMs. HCC-STAR achieved the highest composite quality score and the highest安全通行证safety-pass与 DeepSeek-R1 和 GPT-4o 相比，红色标记选项比例最低（图 2c、2d）。最大的利润是在rate with the lowest proportion of red-flagged options (Fig. 2c, 2d) over DeepSeek-R1 and GPT-4o. The largest margin was in完整性completeness，通过八项覆盖检查表进行评估，需要明确记录分期参考（BCLC或CNLC）、ECOG表现状态、Child-Pugh评分、肿瘤负荷、血管侵犯（包括PVTT分级）、肝外或淋巴结状态、门静脉高压或FLR充分性以及移植资格（补充表8）。完整性上不成比例的裕度反映了 EARL 训练信号：因为复合奖励验证了中间临床标签（表现状态、Child-Pugh 分级、肿瘤负荷、血管侵犯、转移和 BCLC/CNLC/TNM 分期），而不仅仅是最终建议，因此模型被激励准确地呈现人类评估者随后在临床审查期间检查的决定因素。较高的 CoT 分数反映了更完整、符合指南的推理，从而提高了决策路径的可审核性并减少了低价值弯路。在实践中，较高的正确性和安全性评分意味着复杂情况（例如主干 PVTT、显着门静脉高压或 FLR 不足）的不安全选择较少，并且在优先考虑切除或消融、局部治疗或全身治疗时有更明确的理由。这些特性共同支持更安全、更透明的建议，促进护理点的共同决策。, assessed by an eight-item coverage checklist requiring explicit documentation of the staging reference (BCLC or CNLC), ECOG performance status, Child–Pugh score, tumor burden, vascular invasion (including PVTT grade), extrahepatic or nodal status, portal hypertension or FLR adequacy, and transplant eligibility (Supplementary Table 8). The disproportionate margin on completeness mirrors the EARL training signal: because the composite reward verifies intermediate clinical tags (performance status, Child–Pugh grade, tumor burden, vascular invasion, metastasis, and BCLC/CNLC/TNM stage) rather than only the final recommendation, the model is incentivized to surface exactly the determinants that human raters subsequently check for during clinical review. Higher CoT scores reflect more complete, guideline-aligned reasoning, which improves auditability of the decision path and reduces low-value detours. In practice, higher correctness and safety scores translate into fewer unsafe options for complex profiles (for example, main-trunk PVTT, significant portal hypertension, or inadequate FLR) and clearer justification when prioritizing resection or ablation, locoregional therapy, or systemic therapy as appropriate. Together, these properties support safer and more transparent recommendations, facilitating shared decision-making at the point of care.

为了进一步评估模型循证论证的可靠性，三位高级肝胆专家沿着三个维度独立对每项建议的支持证据进行评分（补充部分 B 详细介绍了评分标准）：To further evaluate the reliability of the model’s evidence-based justifications, three senior hepatobiliary specialists independently scored the supporting evidence for each recommendation along three dimensions (Supplementary Section B details the scoring rubric):完整性completeness（所需来源的覆盖范围，包括相关指南部分和证据水平），(coverage of required sources, including relevant guideline sections and evidence levels),正确性correctness（声明与引用来源之间的协议），以及(agreement between the claim and the cited source), and一致性consistency（适合患者的临床情况）。 HCC-STAR 在 60 个病例中在所有三个维度上均取得了最高分，(appropriateness for the patient’s clinical context). HCC-STAR achieved the highest scores across all three dimensions in 60 cases,

4

优于 DeepSeek-R1 和 GPT-4o（扩展数据图 2）。与 CoT 评估一起，这些发现表明该模型提供了更完整、更精确和可重复的证据追踪，支持更安全、更可靠的治疗决策。我们还比较了相同 60 个病例的治疗推荐准确性（图 2e），HCC-STAR 在排名指标上始终优于这两种模型，实现了最高的 Top-1 (79%)、Top-2 (88%) 和 Top-3 (98%) 准确性。这些结果证明了对适当疗法的出色识别以及与专家排名的更接近，表明该模型提供了临床可靠的推理以及更符合指南的治疗优先顺序，有助于弥合法学硕士推理和专家级决策之间的差距。生存歧视消融（扩展数据图 5）提供了直接证据，表明 EARL 阶段的贡献远远超出了 SFT，在外部多中心队列中观察到的收益最大，这与可验证的、排名感知的复合奖励鼓励可转移推理而不是记忆的观点一致。outperforming DeepSeek-R1 and GPT-4o (Extended Data Fig. 2). Together with the CoT evaluation, these findings indicate that the model provides more complete, precise, and reproducible evidence trails, supporting safer and more reliable treatment decision-making. We also compared treatment recommendation accuracy on the same 60 cases (Fig. 2e), and HCC-STAR consistently outperformed both models across ranking metrics, achieving the highest Top-1 (79%), Top-2 (88%), and Top-3 (98%) accuracy. These results demonstrate superior identification of appropriate therapies and closer agreement with expert rankings, indicating that the model delivers clinically reliable reasoning alongside more guideline-concordant treatment prioritization, helping bridge the gap between LLM reasoning and expert-level decision-making. The survival-discrimination ablation (Extended Data Fig. 5) provides direct evidence that the EARL stage contributes substantively beyond SFT, with the largest gains observed on the external multi-center cohort—consistent with the view that the verifiable, ranking-aware composite reward encourages transferable reasoning rather than memorization.

2.3 与医生治疗决策的比较2.3 Comparison with physicians in treatment decision-making

在对 60 个策划案例的经验进行分层的法学硕士和医生的单独比较中，图 2f 显示了参与的住院医生和主治医生的平均准确性。 HCC-STAR 的准确率最接近高级专家，并且比初级和中级医生提供更高的 Top-1 准确率In a separate comparison of LLM and physicians stratified by experience on 60 curated cases, Fig. 2f illustrates the mean accuracy of participating residents and attending physicians. HCC-STAR achieved accuracy closest to that of senior specialists and delivered higher Top-1 accuracy than both junior and intermediate physicians79.2% 对比初级（居民）63.3%；与中级（就读）相比 66.7%79.2% vs. junior (resident) 63.3%; vs. intermediate (attending) 66.7%，并且优于其他法学硕士（最佳法学硕士，70.0%；图 2e）。优势持续保持在 Top-2 位置（, and it outperformed other LLMs (best LLM, 70.0%; Fig. 2e). Advantages persisted at Top-2 (88.3% vs. 初级（居民）72.5%；与中级（就读）相比 80.0%88.3% vs. junior (resident) 72.5%; vs. intermediate (attending) 80.0%）和前 3 名（) and Top-3 (98.3% vs. 初级（居民）72.5%；与中级（就读）相比 83.3%98.3% vs. junior (resident) 72.5%; vs. intermediate (attending) 83.3%）。特别是，Top-1 准确性的提高非常显着，因为第一个推荐的治疗通常指导实际的治疗计划并影响预后。这些成果反映了该模型对关键决策规则的一致应用，包括在满足资格标准时确定切除或消融的候选资格，在适当的时候触发移植转诊，以及抑制与绝对或实际禁忌症相冲突的选项（例如，避免在主干PVTT中进行不适当的TACE）。通过标准化跨病例的规则应用，该模型可以减少合格候选人的治疗不足和不合格患者的过度治疗，从而提高跨医生阶层的敏感性和特异性。). Especially, improvements in Top-1 accuracy were significant because the first recommended therapy often guides the actual treatment plan and influences prognosis. These gains reflect the model’s consistent application of critical decision rules, including identifying candidacy for resection or ablation when eligibility criteria were met, triggering transplant referral when appropriate, and suppressing options that conflicted with absolute or practical contraindications (for example, avoiding inappropriate TACE in main-trunk PVTT). By standardizing rule application across cases, the model can reduce under-treatment in eligible candidates and over-treatment in ineligible patients, thereby improving both sensitivity and specificity across physician strata.

2.4 LLM辅助治疗决策的评价2.4 Evaluation of LLM-assisted treatment decision-making

我们通过比较医生在有模型支持和没有模型支持的情况下的决策，进一步评估了我们的系统作为临床助理的能力，并在图 2f 和图 2g 中报告了相应的结果。当提供我们模型的思维链推理和治疗建议时，几乎所有级别的医生都表现出显着提高的准确性。具体来说，初级（住院医师）将 Top-1 准确率从 63.3% 提高到 73.0% (ΔTop-1 = +9We further assessed our system as a clinical assistant by comparing physicians’ decisions with and without model support and reported the corresponding results in Fig. 2f and Fig. 2g. When provided with our model’s chain-of-thought reasoning and treatment recommendations, physicians at almost all levels demonstrated significantly improved accuracy. Specifically, junior (resident) improved Top-1 accuracy from 63.3% to 73.0% (ΔTop-1 = +9.7).对于中级（主治），无论是否有帮助，Top-1 准确率相似（66.7% 与 63.2%）。 Top-2 和 Top-3 准确度方面的收益非常显着。在 Top-2 中，居民的准确率从 72.5% 提高到 86.8% (ΔTop-2 = +147). For intermediate (attending), Top-1 accuracy was similar with and without assistance (66.7% vs. 63.2%). Gains are prominent at Top-2 and Top-3 accuracy. At Top-2, residents improved accuracy from 72.5% to 86.8% (ΔTop-2 = +14.3) 出席率从 80.0% 提高到 88.3% (ΔTop-2 = +83) and attendings improved from 80.0% to 88.3% (ΔTop-2 = +8.3）。在前三名中，住院医师的准确率提高了 24.6%，主治医师的准确率提高了 7.9%。重要的是，在我们模型的帮助下，初级医生的决策准确性与经验丰富的同事在相同病例上的决策准确性相匹配或超过（例如，人工智能住院医师（73.0% Top-1 准确率）与主治基线（66.7%））。除了准确性的提高之外，决策效率也得到了提高。具体来说，做出治疗决定的平均时间缩短了：居民的决策时间从 26.3 秒减少到 23.5 秒（Δ3). At Top-3, residents’ accuracy increased by 24.6% and attendings’ accuracy increased by 7.9%. Importantly, with our model’s assistance, junior physicians achieved decision accuracy that matched or exceeded that of more-experienced colleagues on the same cases (e.g., Resident with AI (73.0% Top-1 accuracy) vs. Attending baseline (66.7%)). In addition to accuracy gains, decision efficiency also improves. Concretely, the average time to reach a treatment decision is reduced: decision time decreased from 26.3 s to 23.5 s for residents (Δ𝑡 = −2 .8 秒），主治者为 59.2 秒至 27.4 秒（Δ8 s) and from 59.2 s to 27.4 s for attendings (Δ𝑡=−31= −31.8 秒）。这些发现证明了我们的模型作为临床助理的双重好处，提高了决策质量，同时减少了工作量，特别是对于经验不足的医生来说。8 s). These findings demonstrate the dual benefits of our model as a clinical assistant, enhancing decision quality while reducing workload, particularly for less-experienced physicians.

在每个医生层面（图 2g），前后 Top-1 分散显示出明显的对角线上方模式：大多数医生向上移动（在协助下 Top-1 较高）和向左移动（决策时间较短）。住院医生和主治医生的进步都很明显，基线较低的医生的进步最大，而绩效较高的医生的进步较小但一致。例如，如图 4 所示，医生最初倾向于对一名 44 岁男性（ECOG PS 0，Child-Pugh A）进行消融，该男性患有孤立的 1.2×2.0 cm 包膜下 S3 HCC。在 HCC-STAR 的帮助下，该模型的 CoT 提出了符合指南的分期（CNLC Ia / BCLC A / TNM T1a），强调了 III 段的技术可切除性，并为每个选项附加了证据强度（手术切除：1 级，建议 A）。于是医生修改了计划At the per-physician level (Fig. 2g), the pre–post Top-1 scatter shows a clear above-diagonal pattern: most physicians shift upward (higher Top-1 with assistance) and leftward (shorter decision time). Improvements are evident across both residents and attendings, with the largest gains among physicians who started at a lower baseline and smaller but consistent gains among high performers. For instance, as illustrated in Fig. 4, the physician initially favored ablation for a 44-year-old man (ECOG PS 0, Child–Pugh A) with a solitary 1.2×2.0 cm subcapsular S3 HCC. With HCC-STAR assistance, the model’s CoT surfaced guideline-concordant staging (CNLC Ia / BCLC A / TNM T1a), emphasized the technical resectability of segment III, and attached evidence strength to each option (Surgical resection: Level 1, Recommendation A). The physician consequently revised the plan to手术切除surgical resection（地面实况第一线），产生 Top-1 修正并显着减少决策时间（从 78.56 秒到 18.05 秒）。这些结果表明，医生可以从模型突出的决定性线索（例如可切除性、移植优先级、围手术期抗病毒考虑因素）中受益，从而提高决策质量和效率。协助还可以减少医生之间的差异，并消除任何准确性与时间的权衡，从而以更短的决策时间获得更高的准确性。(the ground-truth first-line), yielding a Top-1 correction and a marked reduction in decision time (from 78.56 s to 18.05 s). These results suggest that physicians can benefit from the model’s highlighting of decisive cues (e.g., resectability, transplant deprioritization, perioperative antiviral considerations), thereby improving both decision quality and efficiency. Assistance also reduces between-physician variability and eliminates any accuracy–time trade-off, yielding higher accuracy with shorter decision times.

图1：Figure 1:HCC-STAR 概述：数据管理、模型开发和评估。Overview of HCC-STAR: data curation, model development, and evaluation.a、数据采集、增强和协调。来自 SEER 的 HCC 记录经过清理和标准化，真实世界的 EMR 被标准化为模板。 CNLC 指导、经过临床验证的提示管道将关键变量扩展到指令式 EMR 叙述中。然后，结构化矩阵和叙述被统一到跨源语料库中。, Data acquisition, augmentation, and harmonization. HCC records from SEER are cleaned and normalized, and real-world EMRs are standardized into templates. A CNLC-guided, clinical-validated prompt pipeline expands key variables into instruction-style EMR narratives. Structured matrices and narratives are then unified into a cross-source corpus.b，模型训练。第一阶段：对类似 EMR 的叙述进行临床知识熟悉微调。第 2 阶段：经验累积强化学习，具有临床定制的复合奖励（过程/格式正确性、指南一致的治疗排名、生存估计、简洁性和思想链 (CoT) 质量）。, Model training. Stage 1: Clinical-Knowledge Familiarization Fine-Tuning on EMR-like narratives. Stage 2: Experience-Accrual Reinforcement Learning with a clinically tailored composite reward (process/format correctness, guideline-consistent treatment ranking, survival estimation, brevity, and chain of thought (CoT) quality).c，模型输出。给定 EMR 式输入，HCC-STAR 返回 (i) 类别概率和具有与指南相关的基本原理的排名治疗列表，(ii) 通过分位数校准的基于风险评分的阶段，以及 (iii) 患者特定的生存估计（月）以及风险等级。, Model outputs. Given an EMR-style input, HCC-STAR returns (i) category probabilities and a ranked treatment list with guideline-linked rationales, (ii) a risk score-based stage via quantile calibration, and (iii) a patient-specific survival estimate (months) together with a risk tier.d，模型验证。内部 (SEER) 和外部多中心队列将我们的模型与分期系统（AJCC/TNM、BCLC、CNLC）、代表性法学硕士和经典 ML 进行基准测试，使用 Top-, Model validation. Internal (SEER) and external multi-center cohorts benchmark our model against staging systems (AJCC/TNM, BCLC, CNLC), representative LLMs, and classical ML using Top-𝑘准确性、Harrell 的 C 指数、AUROC 和 Kaplan-Meier 分析。accuracy, Harrell’s C-index, AUROC, and Kaplan–Meier analyses.e，LLM 生成内容的临床比较和评估。我们与医生进行评估并比较三种设置：仅医生、仅法学硕士以及获得法学硕士协助的医生。盲法肝胆专家对 CoT 的完整性、正确性、安全性和循证论证的可靠性进行评分。, Clinical comparison and evaluation of LLM-generated content. We conduct evaluations with physicians and compare three settings: physician only, LLM only, and physicians with LLM assistance. Blinded hepatobiliary specialists score the CoT for completeness, correctness, safety and the reliability of evidence-based justifications.

通过 C 指数进行预后判断。Prognostic discrimination by C-index.在外部多中心队列中（图 3a），HCC-STAR 实现了更高的整体 Harrell 一致性指数（C 指数）（On the external multi-center cohort (Fig. 3a), HCC-STAR achieved a higher overall Harrell’s concordance index (C-index) (0.7371; 95% CI：0.7234–0.75070.7371; 95% CI: 0.7234–0.7507）比每个基于指南的分期系统（集中在 0.66-0.68 左右）要好，并且优于一组强大的通用 LLM（0.70-0.73，包括 Claude、DeepSeek-R1、Gemini-2.5-pro、GPT-5 和 GPT-4o）。两侧成对) than each guideline-based staging system, which clustered around 0.66-0.68, and outperformed a panel of strong general-purpose LLMs (0.70-0.73, including Claude, DeepSeek-R1, Gemini-2.5-pro, GPT-5, and GPT-4o). Two-sided paired𝑃值证实了我们的模型相对于三个基于指南的分期系统具有统计显着性收益（扩展数据表 2）。在 1 年、3 年和 5 年范围内观察到类似的优势，其中 HCC-STAR 在所有方法中始终保持最高的 C 指数。在内部 SEER 测试集（图 3b）上，我们的模型同样实现了卓越的整体辨别力（values confirm the statistically significant gains of our model over three guideline-based staging systems (Extended Data Table 2). Similar advantages were observed at 1-, 3-, and 5-year horizons, where HCC-STAR consistently maintained the highest C-index among all methods. On the internal SEER test set (Fig. 3b), our model likewise achieved superior overall discrimination (0.7079; 95% CI：0.6935–0.72280.7079; 95% CI: 0.6935–0.7228）与 TNM、BCLC 和 CNLC (0.66–0.68) 相比，并且超过了在相同结构化变量（XGBoost、MLP 和 SVM）上训练的经典机器学习基线。报告的 95% 置信区间和) compared with TNM, BCLC, and CNLC (0.66–0.68) and exceeded classical machine-learning baselines trained on the same structured variables (XGBoost, MLP, and SVM). The reported 95% confidence intervals and𝑃值（扩展数据表 3）表明我们的方法具有统计显着性收益。我们进一步比较了监督微调骨干网的 C 指数（扩展数据图 5），揭示了有利于 RL 增强模型的明显且一致的差距，这支持了生存感知强化学习比单独监督微调更能提高预测的观点。总的来说，这些结果表明 HCC-STAR 捕获了离散分期之外的患者水平异质性，并利用连续的、以生存为导向的风险表示来保持跨多中心队列和时间范围的强大概括。values (Extended Data Table 3) demonstrate statistically significant gains of our methods. We further compared C-index across supervised fine-tuned backbones (Extended Data Fig. 5), revealing a clear and consistent gap in favor of the RL-enhanced model, which supports the notion that survival-aware reinforcement learning improves prediction beyond supervised fine-tuning alone. Collectively, these results suggest that HCC-STAR captures patient-level heterogeneity beyond discrete staging and leverages a continuous, survival-oriented risk representation to maintain strong generalization across multi-center cohorts and time horizons.

ROC 的预后判别。Prognostic discrimination by ROC.与时间相关的接收器操作特性分析（扩展数据图 3）进一步证实了这些发现。在 1 年、3 年和 5 年范围内，HCC-STAR 在内部 SEER 测试集和外部多中心队列上均实现了最高的受试者工作特征曲线下面积 (AUROC)，在每个范围内比 TNM、BCLC 和 CNLC 的绝对增益约为 0.07-0.1。在较长的时期内，改善尤其明显，阶段内的异质性变得更加有影响力，固定的指导阈值很难区分低风险和高风险患者。相应的 AUROC 估计、95% 置信区间，以及Time-dependent receiver operating characteristic analyses (Extended Data Fig. 3) further corroborate these findings. Across 1-, 3-, and 5-year horizons, HCC-STAR achieved the highest area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) on both the internal SEER test set and the external multi-center cohort, with absolute gains of roughly 0.07–0.1 over TNM, BCLC, and CNLC at each horizon. Improvements are particularly pronounced at longer horizons, where within-stage heterogeneity becomes more influential and fixed guideline thresholds struggle to separate low- and high-risk patients. Corresponding AUROC estimates, 95% confidence intervals, and𝑃值（扩展数据表 2 和表 3）证实了相对于临床分期系统和竞争性 LLM 基线的统计显着收益。与 C 指数分析一起，这些结果强调，从 HCC 特定数据中学习并通过在与指南一致的目标下的强化学习进行完善的连续的患者水平生存估计比单独的离散指南分期支持更清晰的特定时间区分。values (Extended Data Table 2 and Table 3) confirm statistically significant gains over clinical staging systems and competitive LLM baselines. Together with the C-index analyses, these results highlight that a continuous, patient-level survival estimate that is learned from HCC-specific data and refined by reinforcement learning under guideline-aligned objectives supports sharper time-specific discrimination than discrete guideline staging alone.

对模型预测分期和风险组的 Kaplan-Meier 分析。Kaplan–Meier analyses of model-predicted stagings and risk groups.使用从训练数据导出的基于分位数的阈值（分位数Using quantile-based thresholds derived from the training data (quantile𝐾=3，四个风险等级 A-D），我们的模型对内部和外部队列的生存进行分层。 Kaplan-Meier 曲线表现出从 A 到 D 的清晰、持久、单调的顺序（图 3c、3d），并且在整个随访过程中保持早期分离。置信带在大多数时间范围内显示出相邻层的重叠最小，这表明即使在后续的后续审查中增加了稳健的分离。尽管存在不同的案例组合（例如，外部 B/D 群体更大，内部层次更平衡），但整个数据集的模式是一致的，这表明一组风险阈值可以很好地概括并在分布变化下保持排名。临床上，生存率从A期到D期呈阶梯式下降：A期维持最高生存率，但逐渐下降； B阶段遵循中间过程，与A稳定分离； C/D 阶段表现出明显较高的早期事件发生率，此后存在持续差距。不存在曲线交叉和强对数秩信号（=3, four risk tiers A-D), our model stratified survival in both the internal and external cohorts. The Kaplan–Meier curves exhibit a clear, persistent, monotonic ordering from A to D (Fig. 3c, 3d), with early separation that is maintained throughout follow-up. Confidence bands show minimal overlap for adjacent strata across most time horizons, indicating robust separation even as censoring increases later in follow-up. The pattern is consistent across datasets, despite different case-mixes (e.g., larger B/D groups externally and more balanced strata internally), suggesting that a single set of risk thresholds generalizes well and preserves ranking under distribution shifts. Clinically, survival decreases stepwise from stage A to stage D: stage A maintains the highest survival with a gradual decline; stage B follows an intermediate course with stable separation from A; and stages C/D exhibit markedly higher early event rates with sustained gaps thereafter. The absence of curve crossings and the strong log-rank signal (𝑝< 10−4，在每个面板中注释）与各层的大致成比例的危险一致。, annotated in each panel) are consistent with approximately proportional hazards across strata.

我们使用从训练数据中得出的预先指定的临界值（第 25 个百分位数和第 95 个百分位数）进一步将患者分为低风险组和高风险组，然后将相同的阈值应用于内部 SEER 测试集和外部多中心队列。 Kaplan-Meier 曲线显示两个队列中风险组之间存在明显差异，低风险组的置信带较窄，高风险组的事件发生率明显较高（图 3e、3f）。 2 年时，无进展生存期 (PFS) 为We further stratified patients into low- and high-risk groups using prespecified cutoffs (the 25th and 95th percentiles) derived from the training data and then applied the same thresholds to the internal SEER test set and the external multi-center cohort. The Kaplan–Meier curves display clear divergence between risk groups in both cohorts, with narrow confidence bands for the low-risk group and substantially higher event rates for the high-risk group (Fig. 3e, 3f). At 2 years, progression-free survival (PFS) is74.0%（内部）和(internal) and99.2%（外部）在低风险组中，与(external) in the low-risk group, versus12.4%和and54.0%分别属于高危组。外部和内部队列的风险比为 19.28（95% CI：10.90-34.11；in the high-risk group, respectively. Hazard ratios for the external and internal cohorts are 19.28 (95% CI: 10.90-34.11;𝑃 = 1 .34×1034 × 10−46）和 5.68（95% CI：4.80-6.70；) and 5.68 (95% CI: 4.80-6.70;𝑃 = 4 .92×1092 × 10−108）在低风险组和高风险组之间，表明高风险组的事件风险明显较高，并提供了跨队列的稳健预后分离的有力证据。总之，这些结果支持使用单一决策阈值进行与队列无关的风险分层，并提出了对标记为高风险的患者进行分类、调整监测强度以及优先考虑治疗升级或试验转诊的实际临床实用性。) between the low- and high-risk groups, indicating a markedly higher event risk in the high-risk group and providing strong evidence of robust prognostic separation across cohorts. Together, these results support the use of a single decision threshold for cohort-agnostic risk stratification and suggest practical clinical utility for triage, tailoring surveillance intensity, and prioritizing treatment escalation or trial referral in patients flagged as high risk.

替代治疗建议下的假设 OS 分析。Hypothetical OS analyses under alternative treatment recommendations.为了比较队列水平上治疗建议的预后效果，我们遵循先前的工作[11]，并为我们的模型以及外部队列的 BCLC 和 CNLC 构建了假设的总生存 (OS) 曲线（图 5a）。对于未接受推荐治疗的患者，我们通过从测试集中接受该治疗的患者中随机抽样来估算结果。在这种反事实分配下，我们模型的操作系统中位数是To compare the prognostic effects of treatment recommendations at the cohort level, we followed prior work [11] and constructed hypothetical overall survival (OS) curves for our model and for BCLC and CNLC on the external cohort (Fig. 5a). For patients who did not receive the recommended therapy, we imputed outcomes by random sampling from patients in the test set who did receive that therapy. Under this counterfactual assignment, the median OS for our model is51月，相比之下months, in contrast to29和and32患者分别根据 BCLC 和 CNLC 接受治疗的月数。 Kaplan-Meier 估计表明，与遵守 BCLC 或 CNLC 相比，遵守我们模型的建议与更长的生存期相关。由于结果是根据相似的治疗病例估算的，因此这些估计值容易受到适应症的影响，应被视为假设生成而不是因果关系。这种模式是否会在临床实践中转化为人群水平的益处是一个值得前瞻性研究的问题。months when patients receive treatment according to BCLC and CNLC, respectively. The Kaplan–Meier estimates indicate that adherence to our model’s recommendations is associated with longer survival than adherence to BCLC or CNLC. Because outcomes are imputed from similar treated cases, these estimates are subject to confounding by indication and should be regarded as hypothesis-generating rather than causal. Whether such patterns would translate into population-level benefits in clinical practice is a question that warrants prospective investigation.

按模型定义的风险进行分层产生了一致的模式（图 5b、5c）。在低风险组中，我们模型建议下的假设 OS 在整个随访过程中仍然是最高的，早期曲线分离随着时间的推移而扩大，并且与 BCLC 和 CNLC 相比的对数秩检验具有统计显着性。在高风险组中，所有策略的总生存期均较短。然而，我们的模型仍然产生了一条具有较长尾部的右移曲线，并且差异较小，某些区间仅具有临界显着性。总的来说，这些亚组分析表明，与遵守我们模型的建议相关的潜在益处在各个风险层中都得到维持，并且在基线状况更有利的患者中可能最为明显。Stratification by model-defined risk produced consistent patterns (Fig. 5b, 5c). In the low-risk group, hypothetical OS under our model’s recommendations remained the highest throughout follow-up, with early curve separation that widened over time, and log-rank tests versus BCLC and CNLC are statistically significant. In the high-risk group, overall survival was shorter across all strategies. Yet, our model still yielded a right-shifted curve with a longer tail, and the differences are smaller, with some intervals only being borderline significant. Collectively, these subgroup analyses indicate that the potential benefit associated with adhering to our model’s recommendations is maintained across risk strata and may be most pronounced among patients with more favorable baseline profiles.

2.6 指南更新适应性评价2.6 Evaluation of adaptability to guideline updates

先前的工作确定了具有强大推理能力的模型泛化到新场景的两种机制：验证感知强化学习，能够跨域转移到分布外问题[41]，以及实例化训练数据，为稳健泛化提供上下文基础[42]。这些观察结果与新兴的 SFT-RL 范式一致，其中最近的研究揭示了一个关键区别：监督微调主要编码训练数据中的事实记忆，而强化学习则培养可转移的推理模式 [43]。遵循这一范式，我们用实例化的临床场景对模型进行预处理，以通过 SFT 巩固指南知识，然后在同一场景空间上利用强化学习来开发自适应推理能力。当临床指南的治疗方案或程序规范不断更新时，这种策略尤其有价值。我们的 HCC-STAR 的推理能力可以使模型通过有针对性的系统提示调整来适应这些指南的变化，从而有效地实现知识转移和泛化，而不需要完全重新训练。我们通过肝移植案例说明了这种能力（图 6），其中应用不同的肝移植标准（米兰与 UCSF 标准）仅需要轻量级系统即时细化即可更新推荐的途径及其基本原理，而无需重新训练模型。虽然这个例子作为概念证明，但对不同指南更改场景的更新保真度进行更广泛的定量评估将进一步证实基于提示的适应性，并且是未来工作的自然方向。Prior work identifies two mechanisms by which models with strong reasoning generalize to new scenarios: verificationaware reinforcement learning that enables cross-domain transfer to out-of-distribution problems [41], and instantiated training data that provides contextual grounding for robust generalization [42]. These observations are consistent with the emerging SFT–RL paradigm, where recent studies reveal a critical distinction: supervised fine-tuning primarily encodes factual memorization from training data, while reinforcement learning cultivates transferable reasoning patterns [43]. Following this paradigm, we precondition the model with instantiated clinical scenarios to consolidate guideline knowledge via SFT, then leverage reinforcement learning over the same scenario space to develop adaptive reasoning capabilities. This strategy is particularly valuable when clinical guidelines undergo incremental updates in treatment protocols or procedural specifications. Rather than requiring full retraining, the reasoning capabilities in our HCC-STAR could enable the model to adapt to these guideline changes through targeted system prompt adjustments, effectively achieving knowledge transfer and generalization. We illustrated this capability with a liver transplant case (Fig. 6), where applying different liver transplantation standards (Milan versus UCSF criteria) required only lightweight system prompt refinement to update the recommended pathway and its rationale without retraining the model. While this example serves as a proof of concept, a broader, quantitative evaluation of update fidelity across diverse guideline-change scenarios would further substantiate prompt-based adaptability and is a natural direction for future work.

3 讨论3 Discussion

这项研究提出This study presentsHCC-STAR, 域适应, a domain-adapted推理reasoning该模型将细粒度 HCC 分期、基于证据的精准治疗建议和个体化生存预测统一在一个临床一致的框架内。我们使用基于提示的数据增强策略从 SEER 中合成了 EMR 风格的叙述，该策略根据临床指南和现实世界 EMR 中提炼的临床知识提供信息。我们采用了model that unifies fine-grained HCC staging, evidencebased precision treatment recommendation, and individualized survival prediction within a single, clinically aligned framework. We synthesized EMR-style narratives from SEER using a prompt-based data augmentation strategy informed by distilled clinical knowledge from clinical guidelines and real-world EMRs. We adopted a知识对齐推理knowledge-aligned reasoning具有两个阶段的范例：paradigm with two stages:临床知识熟悉微调 (CKF-FT)Clinical-Knowledge Familiarization Fine-Tuning (CKF-FT)和and经验累积强化学习 (EARL)ExperienceAccrual Reinforcement Learning (EARL)与 GRPO [16] 在临床上with GRPO [16] under a clinically可验证的verifiable复合奖励。该系统提供透明的中间标签和患者级输出，并且在内部 SEER 测试集和来自中国 12 家三级医院的大型外部队列中始终优于主要分期系统和代表性法学硕士，在临床评估和盲法评分方面取得了持续的进步。composite reward. The system surfaces transparent intermediate tags and patient-level outputs and consistently outperforms major staging systems and representative LLMs across an internal SEER test set and a large external cohort from 12 tertiary hospitals across China, with consistent gains in the clinical evaluation and blinded scoring.

首先，该模型表现出对总体生存的卓越区分能力，在内部和外部队列中实现了比 AJCC/TNM、BCLC 和 CNLC 更高的时间依赖性 Harrell 一致性指数 (C-index) 和 AUROC，并且在 1 年、3 年和 5 年终点上持续存在优势。基于模型预测分期和预先指定的低风险组和高风险组的 Kaplan-Meier 分析显示出清晰且单调的分离。这些发现表明，连续的患者水平风险评估保留了基于规则的分期压缩为粗略类别的阶段内异质性。其次，对于治疗建议，系统达到了最高的Top-First, the model demonstrates superior discrimination for overall survival, achieving higher time-dependent Harrell’s concordance index (C-index) and AUROC than AJCC/TNM, BCLC, and CNLC in both internal and external cohorts, with advantages persisting at the 1-, 3-, and 5-year endpoints. Kaplan–Meier analyses based on model-predicted stagings and prespecified low- and high-risk groups show clear and monotonic separation. These findings indicate that continuous, patient-level risk estimation preserves within-stage heterogeneity that rule-based staging compresses into coarse categories. Second, for treatment recommendations, the system attains the highest Top-𝑘相对于指南系统、代表性法学硕士和经典机器学习基线的准确性。 Top-1 的收益最为明显，这具有临床意义，因为一线推荐通常决定实际计划和下游预后。在盲法专家评分中，CoT 的质量提高最多的是accuracy against guideline systems, representative LLMs, and classical ML baselines. Gains are most pronounced for Top-1, which is clinically meaningful because the first-line recommendation typically determines the actual plan and downstream prognosis. In blinded expert scoring, the quality of CoT improves most in完整性completeness并且伴随着更高的安全通过率和更少的危险信号选项，以及更可靠的基于证据的理由。在模型与医生的比较中，该模型的准确率接近高级专家，超过初级和中级医生，并进一步提高了经验不足的临床医生作为助手时的准确性和效率。and is accompanied by higher safety-pass rates and fewer red-flag options with more reliable evidence-based justification. In model-physician comparisons, the model achieves accuracy close to senior specialists, surpassing junior and intermediate physicians, and further enhances the accuracy and efficiency of less experienced clinicians when used as an assistant.

从方法论上讲，有两个要素是核心。首先，Methodologically, two elements are central. First,适应领域的数据合成domain-adapted data synthesis：在协调代码和单位后，经过临床医生验证的提示将结构化条目从 SEER 扩展为 EMR 风格的叙述，并嵌入指南逻辑和简短的证据注释，产生支持下游建模和可审核性的双重表示，并且盲法多维保真度评估量化其临床合理性（扩展数据表 1）。第二，: after harmonizing codes and units, clinician-validated prompts expand structured entries from SEER into EMR-style narratives with embedded guideline logic and brief evidence notes, yielding dual representations that supported downstream modeling and auditability, and a blinded multi-dimensional fidelity assessment quantifies their clinical plausibility (Extended Data Table 1). Second,临床知识和可验证反馈的培训training with clinical knowledge and verifiable feedback：CKF-FT 教授来自工作临床示例的指南一致推理，而不是文本级记忆，而 EARL 优化了可逐步验证的复合奖励（即检查中间临床标签而不仅仅是最终答案）和解耦（即分离治疗排名和生存估计梯度更新以防止多目标干扰）。原则上，这种可验证的、具有排名意识的奖励设计应该转移到其他肿瘤学任务，在这些任务中，可以正式确定以指南为基础的决策规则和排名候选疗法。消融分析（扩展数据图 5）证实了超出监督微调的一致 EARL 增益，外部多中心队列的改进最大。: CKF-FT teaches guideline-consistent reasoning from worked clinical examples rather than text-level memorization, while EARL optimizes a composite reward that is step-verifiable (i.e., inspecting intermediate clinical tags rather than only the final answer) and decoupled (i.e., separating treatment-ranking and survival-estimation gradient updates to prevent multi-objective interference). This verifiable, ranking-aware reward design should, in principle, transfer to other oncology tasks where guideline-anchored decision rules and ranked candidate therapies can be formalized. Ablation analyses (Extended Data Fig. 5) confirm consistent EARL gains beyond supervised fine-tuning, with the largest improvements on the external multi-center cohort.

该系统输出基于风险评分的分期、具有循证原理的治疗排名以及生存估计，这些可以被设计用于多学科团队（MDT）讨论和常规肝病学实践。通过在满足标准时显示切除、消融或移植的资格，并抑制与绝对或实际禁忌症（例如主干 PVTT、明显门脉高压或 FLR 不足）相冲突的选择，该模型可以减少治疗不足和过度治疗。在资源有限且专家访问权限有限的环境中，决策支持可能有助于缩短治疗时间并改善个性化分诊，解决与较差结果相关的现实世界的延误。The system outputs risk score-based staging, ranked therapies with evidence-based rationales, and survival estimation, which can be designed to slot into multidisciplinary team (MDT) discussions and routine hepatology practice. By surfacing eligibility for resection, ablation, or transplant when criteria are met and by suppressing options that conflicted with absolute or practical contraindications such as main-trunk PVTT, marked portal hypertension, or inadequate FLR, the model can reduce both under-treatment and over-treatment. In resource-constrained settings with limited specialist access, decision support may help shorten time-to-treatment and improve individualized triage, addressing real-world delays that are associated with poorer outcomes.

图6：Figure 6:无需再培训即可迅速条件概括治疗建议。我们展示（上）一个具有代表性的 HCC 病例； （中）两种替代系统提示编码肝移植标准（Milan vs. UCSF标准）； （底部）CNLC IV 下模型的思想链，包括候选选项和优先级。通过轻量级提示编辑从米兰切换到 UCSF 会重新排序建议（移植去优先级与优先级）并更新随附的基本原理，同时所有其他案例输入保持固定。Prompt-conditioned generalization of treatment recommendations without retraining. We show (top) a representative HCC case; (middle) two alternative system prompts encoding liver transplantation standards (Milan vs. UCSF criteria); and (bottom) the model’s chain of thought with candidate options and prioritization under CNLC IV. Switching from Milan to UCSF via a lightweight prompt edit reorders the recommendation (transplantation de-prioritized vs. prioritized) and updates the accompanying rationale while all other case inputs remain fixed.这个肝移植例子只是为了说明模型通过系统提示调整的概括，并不参与或替代医学或伦理决策。This liver-transplant example is presented solely to illustrate the model’s generalization via system-prompt adjustments and does not engage in, or substitute for, medical or ethical decision-making.

最近的基于成像和语言的系统通过改善风险分层和决策支持来推进人工智能驱动的肿瘤学[44,29,45]。我们的工作以三种方式扩展了这一领域的工作：针对 HCC 的整个分期、治疗和生存期；对从离散数据合成的 EMR 风格叙述进行培训，并注入符合指南的知识；在临床可验证的奖励下将 CKF-FT 与 EARL 结合起来，以提高推理质量和可移植性。由于指南逻辑是在推理过程中练习而不是记忆，因此可以通过系统提示编辑来合并增量指南更新，而无需模型重新训练。与与固定模式相关并因此仅限于内部集合的经典 ML 模型不同，HCC-STAR 摄取非结构化叙述，保留患者级别的上下文，并且无需重新训练即可对外部站点表现出更强的泛化能力。Recent imaging- and language-based systems have advanced AI-driven oncology by improving risk stratification and decision support [44, 29, 45]. Our work extends this line of work in three ways: targeting HCC across the full continuum of staging, treatment, and survival; training on EMR-style narratives synthesized from discrete data with guideline-aligned knowledge injection; and coupling CKF-FT with EARL under clinically verifiable rewards to enhance reasoning quality and transportability. As the guideline logic is exercised during reasoning rather than memorized, incremental guideline updates can be incorporated via system-prompt edits without model retraining. Unlike classical ML models tied to fixed schemas and therefore restricted to the internal set, HCC-STAR ingests unstructured narratives, preserves patient-level context, and shows stronger generalization to external sites without retraining.

这项研究是回顾性的。尽管在 12 个中心和盲读者研究中进行了验证，但仍需要进行前瞻性试验来量化对临床管理和结果的现实影响，我们将进行前瞻性测试This study is retrospective. Despite validation across 12 centers and blinded reader studies, prospective trials are necessary to quantify the real-world impact on clinical management and outcomes, and we will pursue prospective testing

12

在今后的工作中。假设的总体生存分析依赖于临床相似治疗病例的结果估算，并且容易受到适应症的影响，因为现实世界与指南推荐治疗的偏差往往反映了未记录的临床因素（例如，边缘肝储备、FLR不足、严重门静脉高压）。因此，这些反事实估计应被解释为说明性的而非因果性的。各个中心的文档实践有所不同，部分 EMR 风格的叙述是使用基于提示的数据增强来合成的，这可能会引入相对于本地 EMR 的分布变化。亚组之间的公平性，包括乙型肝炎为主与非乙型肝炎病因和边缘肝储备，值得进一步评估。通用法学硕士发布得很快，与每个新系统进行详尽的比较是不可行的。我们的基线（GPT-5、Gemini-2.5-Pro、GPT-4o、Claude、DeepSeek-R1）与我们的 Qwen3 系列基础模型同时存在，以反映共同的技术视野，并且所提出的知识对齐推理范式预计将在可用时转移到更强大的骨干网。随着指南的发展，需要持续学习或参数高效更新，以在保护隐私的同时保持一致性。in future work. The hypothetical overall-survival analysis relies on outcome imputation from clinically similar treated cases and is vulnerable to confounding by indication, as real-world deviations from guideline-recommended therapy often reflect unrecorded clinical factors (e.g., marginal hepatic reserve, inadequate FLR, severe portal hypertension). These counterfactual estimates should therefore be interpreted as illustrative rather than causal. Documentation practices differed across centers, and part of the EMR-style narratives were synthesized using prompt-based data augmentation, which may introduce the distributional shift relative to native EMRs. Fairness across subgroups, including HBV-predominant versus non-HBV etiologies and marginal hepatic reserve, warrants further evaluation. General-purpose LLMs are released rapidly, and an exhaustive comparison against every new system is infeasible. Our baselines (GPT-5, Gemini-2.5-Pro, GPT-4o, Claude, DeepSeek-R1) were contemporaneous with our Qwen3-series base model to reflect a common technological horizon, and the proposed knowledge-aligned reasoning paradigm is expected to transfer to stronger backbones as they become available. As guidelines evolve, continual learning or parameter-efficient updates will be necessary to maintain alignment while preserving privacy.

该模型的结构化输出和 CoT 提供了与指南一致且基于证据的基本原理，这可能会增加临床医生的信任并促进多学科共识。安全监控应包括禁忌症检查的护栏[24]、边界案例的不确定性线索[46]以及机构级审计跟踪，以确保透明度和问责制。为了衡量现实世界的影响，有必要进行随机或务实试验来评估对治疗选择、治疗时间、生存、生活质量和成本效益的影响。对人类与人工智能团队的研究应该确定何时援助最有利于临床医生以及如何减轻过度或不足的依赖。The model’s structured outputs and CoT provided guideline-consistent and evidence-based rationales, which may increase clinician trust and facilitate multidisciplinary consensus. Safety monitoring should include guardrails for contraindication checks [24], uncertainty cues[46] for borderline cases, and institution-level audit trails to ensure transparency and accountability. To measure real-world impact, randomized or pragmatic trials are necessary to assess the effects on treatment selection, time to treatment, survival, quality of life, and cost-effectiveness. Studies of human–AI teaming should determine when assistance most benefits clinicians and how to mitigate over- or under-reliance.

与此同时，通过视觉语言模型扩展成像和病理学框架可能会进一步提高分期保真度、血管侵犯评估和移植候选资格估计。此外，结合纵向实验室结果和治疗轨迹也可以加强动态风险预测。未来的工作应该评估子群体的公平性，并随着指南的发展制定持续学习策略[47]，包括参数高效学习[48]和隐私保护对齐[49]，这些策略与当地文档风格保持一致，而不需要集中数据。In parallel, extending the framework with imaging and pathology via vision–language models may further improve staging fidelity, vascular invasion assessment, and transplant candidacy estimation. Besides, incorporating longitudinal laboratory results and treatment trajectories could also strengthen dynamic risk prediction. Future work should evaluate subgroup fairness and develop continual-learning strategies [47] as guidelines evolve, including parameter-efficient learning [48] and privacy-preserving alignment [49] that align with local documentation styles without centralizing data.

总之，HCC-STAR 通过透明的推理和强大的外部概括，提供患者级别、符合指南的分期、治疗优先顺序和生存预测。通过统一通常孤立的任务并采用In summary, HCC-STAR delivers patient-level, guideline-consistent staging, treatment prioritization, and survival prediction with transparent reasoning and strong external generalization. By unifying tasks that are typically siloed and by adopting a知识对齐推理knowledge-aligned reasoning临床知识熟悉和积累学习的范式paradigm with clinical-knowledge familiarization and accumulation learning under a clinically可验证的复合奖励verifiable composite reward，该系统推进了人工智能辅助肝病学的发展，并为前瞻性部署和下一代临床决策支持奠定了基础。, the system advances AI-assisted hepatology and lays the groundwork for prospective deployment and next-generation clinical decision support.

4 方法4 Methods

4.1 伦理批准4.1 Ethical approval

这项研究是根据赫尔辛基宣言[50]进行的。使用去识别化患者数据获得了北京清华长庚医院、天津医科大学肿瘤医院、河北医科大学邢台人民医院（25532-4-01）、武汉大学中南医院（2025304K）、浙江大学医学院附属第二医院（2025-1106）、深圳市人民医院机构审查委员会（IRB）的批准(LL-KY-2025247-01)；哈尔滨医科大学第二附属医院(KY2025-088)、广西医科大学第一附属医院(2025-K0373)、哈尔滨医科大学第四附属医院(2025-ethics-33)、北京协和医院(JS-1391)。This study was conducted in accordance with the Declaration of Helsinki [50]. Approval for the use of de-identified patient data was obtained from the institutional review boards (IRBs) of Beijing Tsinghua Changgung Hospital, Tianjin Medical University Cancer Institute and Hospital, and Xingtai People’s Hospital of Hebei Medical University (25532-4-01), Zhongnan Hospital of Wuhan University (2025304K), The Second Affiliated Hospital Zhejiang University School of Medicine (2025-1106), Shenzhen People’s Hospital (LL-KY-2025247-01); The Second Affiliated Hospital of Harbin Medical University (KY2025-088), Guangxi Medical University First Affiliated Hospital (2025-K0373), Fourth Affiliated Hospital of Harbin Medical University (2025-ethics-33), and Peking Union Medical College Hospital (JS-1391).

4.2 数据采集与预处理4.2 Data acquisition and preprocessing

本研究整合了两个互补的数据源：(i) 来自监测、流行病学和最终结果 (SEER) 计划的美国 HCC 数据 [31]，以及 (ii) 从中国 12 家不同地理位置的三级医院收集的真实电子病历 (EMR)。This study integrates two complementary data sources: (i) American HCC data from the Surveillance, Epidemiology, and End Results (SEER) program [31], and (ii) real-world electronic medical records (EMRs) collected from 12 geographically distinct tertiary hospitals across China.

对于 SEER，我们检索了 2004 年至 2020 年间诊断的肝细胞癌 (HCC) 病例，以及人口统计学、病理学和临床变量。人口统计特征包括性别、诊断时年龄和婚姻状况[51]。肿瘤相关变量包括组织学（例如ICD-O-3：8170/3、HCC、NOS）[52]、肿瘤分级、TNM分期[7]、肿瘤大小和每位患者的恶性肿瘤数量。治疗变量包括手术干预、淋巴结手术、放射治疗和其他局部治疗。生物标志物状态（例如，AFP）在可用时进行记录。生存结果源自总生存期 (OS) 和特定原因生存期 (CSS)，以及有关生命状态、生存月数和死亡原因的后续信息。生存数据不完整或治疗文件不明确的病例被排除在外。用于分析的最终 SEER 字段总结在扩展数据表 4 中。For SEER, we retrieved hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) cases diagnosed between 2004 and 2020, together with demographic, pathological, and clinical variables. Demographic features include sex, age at diagnosis, and marital status [51]. Tumor-related variables comprise histology (e.g., ICD-O-3: 8170/3, HCC, NOS) [52], tumor grade, TNM staging [7], tumor size, and the number of malignant tumors per patient. Treatment variables include surgical interventions, lymph node surgery, radiotherapy, and other locoregional therapies. Biomarker status (e.g., AFP) is recorded when available. Survival outcomes are derived from overall survival (OS) and cause-specific survival (CSS), with follow-up information on vital status, survival months, and cause of death. Cases with incomplete survival data or ambiguous treatment documentation are excluded. The final SEER fields used for analysis are summarized in Extended Data Table 4.

与此同时，去识别化的电子病历 (EMR) 提供了反映肝病常规临床实践的细粒度、患者级别的临床详细信息。记录包括东部肿瘤合作组表现状态（ECOG PS）[53]、Child-Pugh 评分[54]以及肝外转移的明确记录。实验室In parallel, de-identified electronic medical records (EMRs) provide fine-grained, patient-level clinical details that reflect routine clinical practice in hepatology. Records include Eastern Cooperative Oncology Group performance status (ECOG PS) [53], Child–Pugh score [54], and explicit documentation of extrahepatic metastasis. Laboratory

13

检查包括全血细胞计数（例如，WBC 6.2 ×10examinations include complete blood counts (e.g., WBC 6.2 ×109/L，血红蛋白125 g/L，血小板190×10/L, hemoglobin 125 g/L, platelets 190 ×109/L）、肝功能测试（胆红素分数、AST、ALT、碱性磷酸酶、GGT、白蛋白、球蛋白）和肿瘤标志物（例如 AFP 445 ng/mL）以及病毒血清学。组织病理学可用于接受肝活检的子集，提供简明的形态学背景。影像学研究通常包括腹部 CT、对比增强 MRI 和超声，以支持诊断和纵向评估。扩展数据表 5 提供了 EMR 字段的结构化摘要。/L), hepatic function tests (bilirubin fractions, AST, ALT, alkaline phosphatase, GGT, albumin, globulin), and tumor markers (e.g., AFP 445 ng/mL), alongside viral serologies. Histopathology is available for the subset undergoing liver biopsy, providing a concise morphologic context. Imaging studies routinely include abdominal CT, contrast-enhanced MRI, and ultrasound to support diagnosis and longitudinal assessment. A structured summary of EMR fields is provided in Extended Data Table 5.

总而言之，SEER 注册表提供了表征 HCC 人群水平模式的大规模结构化数据。相比之下，多中心电子病历可提供患者级别的详细信息，包括实验室、影像和组织病理学评估。这种双源设计能够构建结构化表格和自然语言临床叙述，随后将其协调到教学式培训语料库中，以开发大型语言模型（LLM）。Taken together, the SEER registry provided large-scale structured data characterizing population-level patterns of HCC. In contrast, multi-center EMRs delivered granular patient-level detail, including laboratory, imaging, and histopathology assessments. This dual-source design enabled the construction of structured tables and natural-language clinical narratives, which were subsequently harmonized into instruction-style training corpora for the development of a large language model (LLM).

4.3 外部多中心患者群体4.3 External multi-center patient population

我们收集了来自中国 12 家三级医院的 6,668 名肝细胞癌 (HCC) 患者进行模型外部验证，覆盖 12 个地点：A 中心（北京清华长庚医院）、B 中心（天津医科大学肿瘤医院）、C 中心（武汉大学中南医院）、D 中心（福建医科大学孟超肝胆医院）、E 中心（邢台市人民医院）河北医科大学附属医院）、F中心（浙江大学医学院附属第二医院）、G中心（深圳市人民医院）、H中心（第三军医大学西南医院）、I中心（哈尔滨医科大学第二附属医院）、J中心（广西医科大学附属第一医院）、K中心（哈尔滨医科大学第四附属医院）、L中心（北京协和医学院）医院）。中心级人口统计显示男性占主导地位（5,420/6,668；81.3%）。各个中心的年龄分布是一致的，中位年龄通常在 50 多岁左右（中心中位数的中位数约 59 岁；范围为 52-61 岁）。在现有数据的患者中，65.6%（3,917/5,972）患有肝硬化，19.1%（1,141/5,981）患有糖尿病，28.6%（1,713/5,989）患有高血压。We assembled an external cohort of 6,668 patients with hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) from 12 tertiary hospitals across China for model external validation, spanning 12 sites: Center A (Beijing Tsinghua Changgung Hospital), Center B (Tianjin Medical University Cancer Institute and Hospital), Center C (Zhongnan Hospital of Wuhan University), Center D (Mengchao Hepatobiliary Hospital of Fujian Medical University), Center E (Xingtai People’s Hospital of Hebei Medical University), Center F (The Second Affiliated Hospital Zhejiang University School of Medicine), Center G (Shenzhen People’s Hospital), Center H (Southwest Hospital, Third Military Medical University [Army Medical University]), Center I (The Second Affiliated Hospital of Harbin Medical University), Center J (Guangxi Medical University First Affiliated Hospital), Center K (Fourth Affiliated Hospital of Harbin Medical University), and Center L (Peking Union Medical College Hospital). Center-level demographics show a male predominance (5,420/6,668; 81.3%). Age distributions are consistent across centers, with median ages typically in the late 50s (median of center medians ≈59 years; range, 52–61 years). Among patients with available data, 65.6% (3,917/5,972) have cirrhosis, 19.1% (1,141/5,981) have diabetes, and 28.6% (1,713/5,989) have hypertension.

对于下游评估，我们定义了生存过滤测试集，排除 (i) 围手术期死亡，(ii) 缺少随访的患者，以及 (iii) BCLC、CNLC 或 TNM 分期不完整的病例。结果集包括来自相同 12 个中心的 4,190 名患者，并保留了相似的基线模式（男性 82.5% [3,455/4,190]；肝硬化 70.4% [2,866/4,073 可用]；糖尿病 19.5% [799/4,102 可用]；高血压 29.6% [1,220/4,116 可用]）。扩展数据图 6 描述了患者纳入和排除的流程。扩展数据表 6 和扩展数据表 7 总结了中心特定特征，包括 ECOG PS、Child-Pugh、mALBI、肿瘤负荷、血管侵犯、淋巴结/远处转移和治疗史（例如手术、RFA、TACE、HAIC、EBRT、SIRT、TKI、ICIs）。源数据文件在补充材料中提供。For downstream evaluation, we defined a survival-filtered testing set by excluding (i) perioperative deaths, (ii) patients with missing follow-up, and (iii) cases with incomplete staging under BCLC, CNLC, or TNM. The resulting set comprises 4,190 patients across the same 12 centers and preserved similar baseline patterns (male 82.5% [3,455/4,190]; cirrhosis 70.4% [2,866/4,073 available]; diabetes 19.5% [799/4,102 available]; hypertension 29.6% [1,220/4,116 available]).
