4.4 基于提示和临床医生验证的数据增强4.4 Prompt-based and clinician-validated data augmentation

为了将结构化变量与叙述性临床背景联系起来，并利用大规模 SEER 数据进行法学硕士培训，我们实施了一种基于提示的数据增强策略，该策略以提炼的临床知识（例如治疗指南和循证理由）为指导，这些知识源自《中国原发性肝癌转化和围手术期治疗专家共识》（2024 年版）和清华长庚医院策划的经临床医生验证的 EMR 范例。例如，我们将分期和治疗逻辑形式化为紧凑的决策树，并将它们嵌入到增强提示中（补充清单 1），使推理步骤明确且可验证，以支持可靠的数据生成。肝病专家审查了代表性的 EMR 和提炼的高保真提示模板，这些模板反映了常规记录模式，包括症状排序、实验室和影像学结果的整合、分期的基本原理以及治疗注意事项的讨论。然后，我们以编程方式将 SEER 变量映射到这些模板中，以合成指令式临床叙述，保留真实标签，同时丰富临床语义。使用这些提示，大型语言模型（例如 GPT-4o）生成类似 EMR 的出院摘要、分期解释和治疗注释，其中包含关键的人口统计、肿瘤和结果信息。To link structured variables with narrative clinical contexts, and to leverage large-scale SEER data for LLM training, we implemented a prompt-based data augmentation strategy guided by distilled clinical knowledge (such as treatment guidelines and evidence-based justifications) that is derived from both the Chinese Expert Consensus on Conversion and Perioperative Therapy of Primary Liver Cancer (2024 edition) and clinician-validated EMR exemplars curated at Tsinghua Changgung Hospital. For example, we formalized staging and treatment logic into compact decision trees and embedded them in the augmentation prompts (Supplementary Listing 1), making the reasoning steps explicit and verifiable to support reliable data generation. Hepatology specialists reviewed representative EMRs and distilled high-fidelity prompt templates that reflect routine documentation patterns, including symptom sequencing, integration of laboratory and imaging findings, the rationale for staging, and the discussion of treatment considerations. We then programmatically mapped SEER variables into these templates to synthesize instruction-style clinical narratives that preserved ground-truth labels while enriching clinical semantics. Using these prompts, a large language model (e.g., GPT-4o) generated EMR-like discharge summaries, staging interpretations, and treatment notes that incorporate key demographic, tumor, and outcome information.

统一数据集以两种互补格式表示：(i) 来自 SEER 的结构化表格数据，用于基线统计建模；(ii) 适合法学硕士培训的综合叙述 EMR。图 1a 展示了整个预处理工作流程，包括数据清理、基于提示的增强以生成 EMR 风格的叙述，以及跨源协调以获得结构化和自然语言记录。补充材料提供了一个用于 LLM 培训的综合 EMR 样式模板（清单 2）和数据增强提示（清单 3）。The harmonized dataset was represented in two complementary formats: (i) structured tabular data from SEER for baseline statistical modeling and (ii) synthetic narrative EMRs suitable for LLM training. Fig. 1a presents the overall pre-processing workflow, including data cleaning, prompt-based augmentation to produce EMR-style narratives, and cross-source harmonization to obtain both structured and natural-language records. The Supplementary provided a synthetic EMR-style template for LLM training (Listing 2) and the data augmentation prompt (Listing 3).

4.5 综合 EMR 叙述的定量保真度评估4.5 Quantitative fidelity assessment of synthetic EMR narratives

为了解决即时增强的 EMR 叙述可能与真实临床文档存在偏差的担忧，我们对合成语料库进行了定量、盲法保真度评估。从增强训练语料库中，我们随机抽取了 140 个合成 EMR，在 CNLC 各个阶段进行分层，以确保早期、中期、To address the concern that prompt-augmented EMR narratives may diverge from real clinical documentation, we performed a quantitative, blinded fidelity assessment of the synthetic corpus. From the augmented training corpus, we randomly sampled 140 synthetic EMRs, stratified across CNLC stages to ensure balanced coverage of early, intermediate,

14

和晚期疾病。三名资深肝胆临床医生均拥有至少 10 年的临床经验，对模型身份和病例来源不知情，使用五点李克特量表沿着六个预先指定的维度对每个 EMR 进行独立评分（1 = 高度不合理；5 = 完全合理；NA = 不适用）。每当评分者给出 3 分或以下的分数时，他们都需要提供自由文本的理由，并在数字分数旁边保留故障模式的定性证据。and advanced disease. Three senior hepatobiliary clinicians, each with at least 10 years of clinical experience and blinded to model identity and case origin, independently scored each EMR along six pre-specified dimensions using a five-point Likert scale (1 = highly unreasonable; 5 = fully reasonable; NA = not applicable). Raters were required to provide a free-text rationale whenever they assigned a score of 3 or below, preserving qualitative evidence of failure modes alongside the numeric scores.

六个维度旨在询问领域级的合理性和跨领域的一致性：(i) 以其他 EMR 领域为条件的指定 ECOG 绩效状态的合理性； (ii) 鉴于实验室和影像学结果，Child-Pugh 分级的合理性； (iii) 肝外转移标志与影像报告之间的一致性； (iv) 实验室小组的内部一致性及其与原发性肝癌临床特征的兼容性； (v) 成像报告相对于常规放射学记录的真实性； (vi) 所有领域的全球叙述一致性作为一个单一的临床故事。补充图 8 中提供了完整的评分界面。The six dimensions were designed to interrogate both field-level plausibility and cross-field coherence: (i) plausibility of the assigned ECOG performance status conditional on other EMR fields; (ii) plausibility of the Child–Pugh grade given the laboratory and imaging findings; (iii) consistency between the extrahepatic-metastasis flag and the imaging report; (iv) internal consistency of the laboratory panel and its compatibility with the clinical profile of primary liver cancer; (v) realism of the imaging report relative to routine radiologic documentation; and (vi) global narrative consistency across all fields as a single clinical story. The full scoring interface is provided in Supplementary Fig.8.

我们通过三种方式总结保真度。首先，我们报告了每个维度的平均和中位数 Likert 评分，以及获得 4 或 5 分（被视为临床可接受）的样本比例。其次，我们使用 Krippendorff 的 alpha（五点李克特分数的间隔距离）计算所有评分者和维度的评分者间一致性，并另外报告每个维度的组内相关系数（ICC(2,k)、双向随机效应、平均测量）。第三，我们将一个或多个评估者对任何维度打分 1 或 2 的任何 EMR 标记为候选低保真度案例；对这些病例进行了重新检查，以表征系统性故障模式（例如，实验室成像不连贯或难以置信的 Child-Pugh 分配）。扩展数据表 1 总结了汇总结果。简而言之，2,518 个非 NA 维度级别评级的总体保真度得分为 4.81 ± 0.55，96.0% 的评级在临床上可接受（分数≥4）。使用精确一致性、一点内一致性、Krippendorff’s 来评估评估者间的一致性We summarized fidelity in three ways. First, we reported the mean and median Likert score per dimension, together with the proportion of samples receiving a score of 4 or 5 (regarded as clinically acceptable). Second, we computed inter-rater agreement using Krippendorff’s alpha (interval distance for five-point Likert scores) across all raters and dimensions, and additionally reported intraclass correlation coefficients (ICC(2,k), two-way random-effects, average measures) for each dimension. Third, we flagged any EMR in which one or more raters assigned a score of 1 or 2 to any dimension as a candidate low-fidelity case; these cases were re-examined to characterize systematic failure modes (for example, laboratory–imaging incoherence or implausible Child–Pugh assignment). Aggregate results are summarized in Extended Data Table 1. In brief, the overall fidelity score was 4.81 ± 0.55 across 2,518 non-NA dimension-level ratings, and 96.0% of ratings were clinically acceptable (score ≥4). Inter-rater consistency was assessed using exact agreement, within-one-point agreement, Krippendorff’s𝛼和 ICC(2,k)； NA 评级被排除在相应的分母之外。, and ICC(2,k); NA ratings were excluded from the corresponding denominators.

4.6 基于LLM的系统的开发4.6 Development of LLM-based system

我们开发了一个基于法学硕士的框架，用于分期、治疗推荐和生存预测，该框架采用We developed an LLM-based framework for staging, treatment recommendation, and survival prediction that employs a知识对齐推理机制knowledge-aligned reasoning mechanism而不是rather than指导方针的文本级记忆text-level memorization of guidelines。我们没有对逐字指导文件进行培训，而是构建了. Instead of training on verbatim guideline documents, we constructed临床实例worked clinical examples通过基于提示的增强，将指南逻辑和证据引用注入现实的 EMR 风格的叙述中。我们训练模型充当肝胆临床医生，并根据诊断记录、影像报告、手术总结和出院文件进行推理，从而通过重复练习而不是文本记忆来内化与指南一致的逻辑。这种基于现实世界案例、推理优先的设计增强了情境化，并支持跨外部数据集和任务的稳健泛化，正如之前的工作也证明了强化学习在跨规则变体文本和视觉设置获取泛化知识方面的能力一样[55, 41]。图 1 总结了数据流以及模型开发和验证。与完全依赖结构化特征的传统机器学习方法（例如多层感知器和 XGBoost）相比，我们的框架直接摄取异构临床叙述。这种端到端的表述鼓励模型揭示临床显着因素，将推理步骤链接到与指南一致的行动，并使中间变量明确以在强化学习期间进行验证和奖励塑造。via prompt-based augmentation that injects guideline logic and evidence citations into realistic EMR-style narratives. We trained the model to behave as a hepatobiliary clinician and to reason across diagnostic notes, imaging reports, operative summaries, and discharge documentation, thereby internalizing guideline-consistent logic through repeated practice instead of text memorization. This real-world case-based, reasoning-first design enhanced contextualization and supported robust generalization across external datasets and tasks, just as prior works also demonstrated the capability of reinforcement learning in acquiring generalizable knowledge across rule-variant textual and visual settings [55, 41]. Fig. 1 summarizes the data flow as well as model development and validation. Compared with conventional machine learning methods (e.g., multilayer perceptrons and XGBoost) that rely exclusively on structured features, our framework directly ingests heterogeneous clinical narratives. This end-to-end formulation encourages the model to surface clinically salient factors, chain reasoning steps to guideline-aligned actions, and make intermediate variables explicit for verification and reward shaping during reinforcement learning.

4.7 模型选择和临床知识熟悉微调4.7 Model selection and clinical-knowledge familiarization fine-tuning

在临床培训中，医生并不是从高风险的决策开始，而是通过代表性病例、掌握术语、分期线索、治疗意图和粗略预后模式来熟悉临床背景和指南逻辑。受这一进展的推动，我们引入了一个反映这一过程的临床知识熟悉阶段，要求模型在相同的约束下进行推理，而不是记住措辞。我们将这个阶段定义为In clinical training, physicians do not begin with high-stakes decision-making but build familiarity with the clinical context and guideline logic by working through representative cases, mastering terminology, staging cues, treatment intent, and coarse prognostic patterns. Motivated by this progression, we introduced a clinical-knowledge familiarization phase that mirrored this process, requiring the model to reason under the same constraints rather than memorize phrasing. We frame this stage as临床知识熟悉程度微调clinical-knowledge familiarization fine-tuning：该模型预先读取（即浏览和处理）大量 EMR 式病例，以获取肝细胞癌的基本知识，包括术语、分期线索、治疗意图和粗略预后模式，然后再进行细粒度决策优化。: the model pre-reads (i.e., skims and works through) a large volume of EMR-style cases to acquire basic knowledge of hepatocellular carcinoma, including terminology, staging cues, treatment intent, and coarse prognostic patterns, before engaging in fine-grained decision optimization.

我们探索了三个大型模型系列，包括 Qwen3-8B/32B [39]、QWQ-32B [40] 和 DeepSeek-R1 [38]，它们因其强大的中国生物医学能力和强大的推理能力而被选中。每个模型均通过监督微调 (SFT) 在约 20,000 个指令-响应对的精选语料库上进行调整。该语料库集成了 (i) 与指南一致的综合推理案例，(ii) 从现实世界的 EMR 中提取的叙述，以及 (iii) SEER 衍生的增强映射，以便将指南逻辑和证据引用嵌入到现实的临床背景中，而不是以抽象散文的形式呈现。We explored three families of large-scale models, including Qwen3-8B/32B [39], QWQ-32B [40], and DeepSeek-R1 [38], which were chosen for their strong Chinese biomedical capabilities and robust reasoning. Each model was adapted via supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on a curated corpus of approximately 20,000 instruction–response pairs. The corpus integrates (i) guideline-aligned synthetic reasoning cases, (ii) narratives distilled from real-world EMRs, and (iii) SEER-derived augmented mappings, so that guideline logic and evidence citations are embedded within the realistic clinical context rather than presented as abstract prose.

15

形式上，给出输入案例描述Formally, given an input case description𝑥及其目标输出and its target output𝑦（治疗建议和预后信息，包括生存月数），监督微调目标是令牌级交叉熵：(treatment recommendations and prognostic information, including survival months), the supervised fine-tuning objective is the token-level cross-entropy:

哪里where𝑇表示输出长度，denotes the output length,𝑥是输入记录，is the input record,𝑦𝑡是步骤中的参考标记is the reference token at step𝑡, 和, and𝜋𝜃𝜋 𝜃是参数化的语言模型is the language model parameterized by𝜃。这一目标鼓励模型在强化学习之前模仿基于指南的推理，提供稳定的初始化来减轻奖励剥削。. This objective encouraged the model to imitate guideline-based reasoning before reinforcement learning, providing a stable initialization that mitigated reward exploitation.

为了促进后期的强化学习，我们采用了To facilitate reinforcement learning in later stages, we employed结构化提示structured prompting并要求模型生成中间标签，例如and required the model to generate intermediate tags such as<ps> , <child_pugh>, 和, and<tumor_size>。这种设计实现了自动奖励计算，同时保持了人工验证的透明度。我们还在提示中嵌入了一个详细的示例，以提高异质患者病例的泛化能力。我们在 NVIDIA B200 GPU 上以 5e-6 的学习率进行了三个时期的临床知识熟悉微调，使用梯度检查点 [56] 和混合精度训练 [57] 来增强内存和计算效率。. This design enabled automatic reward computation while maintaining transparency for human verification. We also embedded an elaborate example into the prompt to improve generalization across heterogeneous patient cases. We performed clinical-knowledge familiarization fine-tuning on NVIDIA B200 GPUs for three epochs at a learning rate of 5e-6, using gradient checkpointing [56] and mixed-precision training [57] to enhance memory and computational efficiency.

4.8 法学硕士的经验累积强化学习4.8 Experience-Accrual reinforcement learning for LLMs

临床知识熟悉微调（CKF-FT）类似于临床培训的早期阶段，初级学员使用教科书般但真实的案例来巩固基础知识。在下一阶段，我们试图模仿住院医师风格的实践，学习者关注细节，整合患者的具体特征，并用证据证明每项建议的合理性。强化学习已被证明可以通过特定任务的奖励来优化行为，从而强化 SFT 之外的推理 [58,16,59,14]。因此，我们执行了Clinical–Knowledge Familiarization Fine–Tuning (CKF–FT) is analogous to the early phase of clinical training, in which a junior trainee consolidates foundational knowledge using textbook-like yet realistic cases. In the next stage, we sought to mirror residency-style practice, where the learner attends to details, integrates patient-specific features, and justifies each recommendation with evidence. Reinforcement learning has been shown to strengthen reasoning beyond SFT [58, 16, 59, 14] by optimizing behavior with task-specific rewards. Therefore, we performed经验累积强化学习（EARL）Experience-Accrual Reinforcement Learning (EARL)通过多维度、可验证的奖励设计，增强模型的临床理解和推理能力。在 EARL 的领导下，该模型接收了细粒度的病例级反馈，关注微妙的禁忌症和合并症，生成基于证据的基本原理，对治疗方法进行适当排序而不是归结为单一选项，并通过结果感知反馈学习个体化预后映射。这种从熟悉临床知识到积累经验的学习轨迹与现实世界的培训平行，反映了从初级医师到高级医师的进步。with a multi-dimensional and verifiable reward design to enhance the model’s clinical understanding and reasoning abilities. Under EARL, the model received fine-grained case-level feedback, attended to subtle contraindications and comorbidities, generated evidence-based rationales, ranked therapies appropriately rather than collapsing onto a single option, and learned individualized prognostic mappings via outcome-aware feedback. This learning trajectory, from clinical knowledge familiarization to experience accrual, parallels real-world training and reflects the progression from junior to senior physician.

基于不同LLM的监督微调性能（扩展数据图4），我们确定Qwen332B由于其优越的临床知识对齐性能作为推理模型的骨干，并随后在复合奖励下对其进行优化。为了实现 HCC 的精准治疗，我们设计了一种可验证的多维奖励，专门针对符合指南的治疗建议和生存预测。与仅评估正确性的单目标奖励相比，我们的复合设计联合评估Based on the supervised fine-tuning performance of different LLMs (Extended Data Fig. 4), we determined Qwen332B as the backbone of the reasoning model owing to its superior clinical knowledge alignment performance, and subsequently optimized it under a composite reward. To enable precision therapy in HCC, we designed a verifiable, multidimensional reward tailored to guideline-consistent treatment recommendations and survival prediction. In contrast to single-objective rewards that only evaluate correctness, our composite design jointly evaluated准确度accuracy , 临床可解释性clinical interpretability , 指南一致性guideline consistency, 和, and临床证据clinical evidence，鼓励更好地反映现实世界临床推理的输出。 RL 目标在学习策略下最大化预期奖励：, encouraging outputs that better reflect real-world clinical reasoning. 组相关策略优化 (GRPO)。Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO).我们采用了组相对策略优化（GRPO）[16]，这是一种 PPO 风格的 [60] 方法，无需价值模型即可将策略与复合奖励保持一致。对于每个提示We adopted Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) [16], a PPO-style [60] method that aligned the policy with the composite reward without a value model. 继 PPO 之后，我们使用了代币级别的重要性比率Following PPO, we used a token-level importance ratio

并优化了对候选者和代币进行平均的修剪代理，并使用 KL 惩罚来限制与冻结参考策略的漂移and optimized a clipped surrogate averaged over candidates and tokens, with a KL penalty that constrained drift from a frozen reference policy𝜋参考（从 SFT 初始化）：ref (initialized from SFT):

该目标通过裁剪保留了 PPO 的信任域行为，消除了对价值模型的需求，并应用组相对缩放来提供低方差学习信号，该信号能够可靠地区分相同输入的高质量和低质量代。这些特性在具有审查结果和部分监督的异构临床任务中特别有价值。This objective preserved PPO’s trust-region behavior through clipping, eliminated the need for a value model, and applied group-relative scaling to deliver a low-variance learning signal that reliably distinguished high- and low-quality generations for the same input. These properties were especially valuable in heterogeneous clinical tasks with censored outcomes and partial supervision.

解耦优化策略。Decoupled optimization strategy.为了增强异构目标的训练稳定性，我们采用了解耦优化策略，其中治疗相关和生存相关的奖励在单独的 GRPO 更新中进行优化。每个奖励都会产生一个独立的梯度，我们随后在策略优化期间聚合更新。这种方法减轻了任务之间的梯度干扰，保留了特定于任务的优化动态，并且仍然允许模型从联合多目标强化学习中受益。To enhance training stability with heterogeneous objectives, we adopted a decoupled optimization strategy in which treatment-related and survival-related rewards were optimized in separate GRPO updates. Each reward produced an independent gradient, and we subsequently aggregated the updates during policy optimization. This approach mitigated gradient interference between tasks, preserved task-specific optimization dynamics, and still allowed the model to benefit from joint multi-objective reinforcement learning.

4.9 推理数据综合4.9 Reasoning data synthesis

与临床指南和专家共识保持一致。Alignment with clinical guidelines and expert consensus.我们严格按照提示设计We designed prompts in strict accordance with the原发性肝癌诊治指南（CNLC，2024年版）Guidelines for the Diagnosis and Treatment of Primary Liver Cancer (CNLC, 2024 Edition)[32]通过结构化临床推理支持强化学习。每个提示都指示模型充当高级肝胆临床研究员，评估患者信息，包括体能状态、Child-Pugh 评分、肿瘤负荷、转移和影像学结果，并提供与指南决策树一致的分步推理。补充清单 4 中提供了提示设计。[32] to support reinforcement learning with structured clinical reasoning. Each prompt instructed the model to act as a senior hepatobiliary clinical researcher, evaluate patient information, including performance status, Child–Pugh score, tumor burden, metastasis, and imaging findings, and provide step-by-step reasoning aligned with the guideline decision tree. The prompt design was provided in Supplementary Listing 4.

为了模仿现实世界的临床决策，列举了涵盖所有潜在治疗方式的提示，包括手术切除、消融、肝移植、TACE、全身治疗、放射治疗、支持治疗和姑息治疗。该模型需要为每个选项分配 0 到 1 之间的连续适用性分数，并附有与指南决策树一致的理由。我们通过要求输出明确引用来进一步将思想链推理与专家共识结合起来To mimic real-world clinical decision-making, prompts were enumerated to encompass all potential treatment modalities, including surgical resection, ablation, liver transplantation, TACE, systemic therapy, radiotherapy, supportive care, and palliative care. The model was required to assign each option a continuous suitability score between 0 and 1, accompanied by a justification consistent with the guideline decision tree. We further aligned the chain-of-thought reasoning with expert consensus by requiring outputs to explicitly reference适应症、禁忌症、证据级别和推荐等级indications, contraindications, levels of evidence, and recommendation grades。除了列出可行的疗法之外，该模型还被要求比较指南推荐的选项并阐明对它们进行排名的理由。例如，在早期单发肿瘤（≤3 cm）中，根据报告的生存获益，切除优先于消融（证据级别1，建议A），而移植由于供体稀缺和公平性考虑而被降级（证据级别3，建议B）。. Beyond listing feasible therapies, the model was asked to compare guideline-recommended options and articulate the rationale for ranking them. For instance, in early-stage single tumors (≤3 cm), resection was prioritized over ablation based on reported survival benefits (evidence level 1, recommendation A), while transplantation was downgraded due to donor scarcity and fairness considerations (evidence level 3, recommendation B).

结构化且经过验证的标记。Structured and verified tagging.我们强制执行结构化标签以促进强化学习期间的自动评估，并在补充清单 5 中提供了具体提示。临床变量如 PS、Child-Pugh 评分、肿瘤大小、肿瘤数量、转移状态和血管侵犯均使用 XML 样式标签进行注释（例如，We enforced structured tagging to facilitate automatic evaluation during reinforcement learning, and a specific prompt was provided in Supplementary Listing 5. Clinical variables such as PS, Child–Pugh score, tumor size, number of tumors, metastasis status, and vascular invasion were annotated with XML-style tags (e.g.,<ps> , <child_pugh> , <tumor_size>）。这确保了奖励分配的可靠解析和临床医生的透明度，临床医生可以验证模型输出引用了适当的临床证据。). This ensured both reliable parsing for reward assignment and transparency for clinicians, who could verify that model outputs referenced appropriate clinical evidence.

与生存相关的字段（包括死亡原因和生存月数）包含在输入中，但在生成过程中被明确屏蔽。该模型需要预测生存状态并提供合理性，而不直接引用真实结果，这与涉及审查的标准生存分析设置一致[61, 62]。这允许相同的数据集支持两者Survival-related fields, including cause of death and survival months, were included in the input but explicitly masked during generation. The model was required to predict survival status and provide justification without directly referencing ground-truth outcomes, consistent with standard survival analysis settings involving censoring [61, 62]. This allowed the same dataset to support both治疗推荐奖励treatment recommendation rewards和and生存预测奖励survival prediction rewards，形成监督微调和强化学习的基础。, forming the basis for supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning.

By combining continuous scoring, structured outputs, and interpretable reasoning, the framework closely coupled guideline-consistent decision making with reinforcement-learning objectives and ensured both reliability and trainability under the GRPO algorithm.

4.10 与竞争基线的比较4.10 Comparison with competitive baselines

为了严格评估治疗建议和生存预后的表现，我们设计了两项补充评估。首先，我们将所提出的基于法学硕士的系统与传统的机器学习（ML）方法以及开源和商业法学硕士进行了比较。其次，我们针对反映不同临床指南的广泛使用的分期系统（AJCC (TNM)、BCLC 和 CNLC）对预后判别进行了基准测试。我们使用 Top- 测量了治疗建议To rigorously assess performance in treatment recommendation and survival prognosis, we designed two complementary evaluations. First, we compared the proposed LLM-based system with traditional machine-learning (ML) methods and with open-source and commercial LLMs. Second, we benchmarked prognostic discrimination against widely used staging systems (AJCC (TNM), BCLC, and CNLC) that reflect different clinical guidelines. We measured treatment recommendation using Top-𝑘准确性，我们使用时间相关的 Harrell 一致性指数（C 指数）和跨多个视野的 ROC 曲线，以及风险分层的 Kaplan-Meier 分析来评估生存预后。accuracy, and we evaluated survival prognosis using time-dependent Harrell’s concordance index (C-index) and ROC curves across multiple horizons, together with Kaplan–Meier analyses for risk stratification.

对于 ML 基线，我们实现了代表性的经典方法、概率方法和神经方法，包括支持向量机 (SVM) [63]、XGBoost [64]、贝叶斯模型 [65] 和多层感知器 (MLP) [66]。这些模型根据从 SEER 中提取的结构化变量（例如实验室标志物、肿瘤大小、血管侵犯和肝外转移）进行训练，以共同预测治疗类别和生存时间。由于传统的机器学习方法依赖于手动抽象的特征和固定的变量模式，因此这些基线无法推广到外部多中心队列，其中格式和变量可用性与 SEER 不同。因此，与 ML 方法的比较仅限于内部 SEER 测试集。相比之下，我们的法学硕士直接处理非结构化的 EMR 叙述，保留患者级别的详细信息，并捕获肿瘤负荷、肝功能、血管侵袭和治疗模式之间的非线性相互作用。法学硕士还产生了明确的思维链推理，为临床解释提供了透明的支持。For the ML baselines, we implemented representative classical, probabilistic, and neural approaches, including support vector machines (SVMs) [63], XGBoost [64], Bayesian models [65], and multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) [66]. These models were trained on structured variables extracted from SEER (e.g., laboratory markers, tumor size, vascular invasion, and extrahepatic metastasis) to jointly predict treatment categories and survival time. Because traditional ML methods depend on manually abstracted features and fixed variable schemas, these baselines cannot generalize to the external multi-center cohort, where formats and variable availability differ from SEER. Consequently, comparisons with ML methods were restricted to the internal SEER test set. In contrast, our LLM directly processed unstructured EMR narratives, preserved patient-level detail, and captured nonlinear interactions among tumor burden, liver function, vascular invasion, and treatment patterns. The LLM also generated explicit chain-of-thought reasoning that provided transparent support for clinical interpretation.

4.11 指标4.11 Metrics

我们使用 Top- 测量了治疗建议We measured treatment recommendation using Top-𝑘准确性，我们使用 Harrell 一致性指数 (C-index) 和 ROC 曲线下时间依赖性面积 (AUROC) 评估 1 年、3 年和 5 年的预后。使用模型预测的分期和预先指定的低/高风险截止值，通过 Kaplan-Meier 分析对风险分层进行量化。accuracy, and we assessed prognosis with Harrell’s concordance index (C-index) and time-dependent area under the ROC curve (AUROC) at 1, 3, and 5 years. Risk stratification was quantified with Kaplan–Meier analyses using model-predicted stagings and prespecified low/high-risk cutoffs.

通过构造，SimBy construction, Sim𝑘 ( 𝑥) ε[0) ∈[0,1]，仅当 Top- 时才等于 11], it equals 1 only when the Top-𝑘项目在成员资格和顺序上完全匹配，并且随着重叠缩小或等级分歧而减少。items match exactly in membership and order, and it decreases as overlap shrinks or ranks diverge.

预后分层：Prognosis stratification:Harrell 的 C 指数衡量了相对于观察到的生存率和审查而言正确排序的患者对的比例，这反映了随访的全球排名表现。 1、3 和 5 年的时间依赖性 AUROC 通过将模型的连续风险视为分数并评估每个时间点处于风险中的病例对照可分离性来量化特定范围的歧视。我们报告了总体 C 指数和特定水平 C 指数（如所示），以及相同水平的 AUROC 值。Harrell’s C-index measures the proportion of correctly ordered patient pairs with respect to observed survival and censoring, which reflects global ranking performance over follow-up. Time-dependent AUROC at 1, 3, and 5 years quantifies horizon-specific discrimination by treating the model’s continuous risk as a score and evaluating case–control separability among those at risk at each time point. We reported the overall C-index and horizon-specific C-index where indicated, together with AUROC values at the same horizons.

卡普兰-迈耶：Kaplan–Meier:我们通过以下方式生成生存曲线We generated survival curves by模型预测分期model-predicted staging(A–D) 使用在训练队列中学习到的基于分位数的阈值，并在不改变的情况下转移到测试队列，并且通过(A–D) using quantile-based thresholds learned on the training cohort and transferred without change to the test cohorts, and by风险组risk group使用预先指定的低/高截止值。曲线总结了一段时间内的事件轨迹，并支持中位生存率和群体分离的比较。using a prespecified low/high cutoff. Curves summarize event trajectories over time and support comparisons of median survival and group separation.

替代治疗建议下的假设 OS 分析：Hypothetical OS analyses under alternative treatment recommendations:为了比较队列水平的治疗算法，我们通过将每位患者分配给推荐的治疗来构建假设的总体生存曲线To compare treatment algorithms at the cohort level, we constructed hypothetical overall-survival curves by assigning each patient to the therapy recommended

19

通过给定的算法（我们的模型或临床分期方案）。对于未接受推荐治疗的患者，我们通过从测试集中接受该治疗的临床相似病例中重新采样来估算结果。然后估计 Kaplan-Meier 曲线，并报告每种算法下的中位 OS，以反映人群水平结果的潜在差异。by a given algorithm (either our model or a clinical staging scheme). For patients who did not receive the recommended therapy, we imputed outcomes by resampling from clinically similar cases within the test set who did receive that therapy. Kaplan–Meier curves were then estimated, and median OS under each algorithm was reported to reflect potential differences in population-level outcomes.

4.12 临床评价的研究设计和参与者4.12 Study design and participants for clinical evaluation

我们设计了一项全面的临床研究，包含三个部分：（i）人类专家评估框架来评估LLM生成内容的质量，（ii）直接比较LLM系统与不同资历级别的医生之间的诊断准确性，以及（iii）评估模型在协助初级和中级医生制定治疗决策方面的有效性。招募13名不同临床经验水平的肝胆科医生，其中具有1-5年执业经验的初级（住院）医师3名，具有5-10年经验的中级（主治）医师4名，10年以上经验的高级医师6名。本研究经北京清华长庚医院医学科学研究伦理委员会批准（IRB 25532-4-01）。We designed a comprehensive clinical study that incorporates three components: (i) a human expert evaluation framework to assess the quality of LLM-generated content, (ii) a direct comparison of diagnostic accuracy between the LLM system and physicians at different levels of seniority, and (iii) an assessment of the model’s effectiveness in assisting junior and intermediate physicians in treatment decision-making. A panel of 13 hepatobiliary physicians with varying levels of clinical experience is recruited, comprising three junior (resident) physicians with 1–5 years of practice experience, four intermediate (attending) physicians with 5–10 years of experience, and six senior physicians with more than 10 years of experience. This study was approved by the Beijing Tsinghua Changgung Hospital Medical Science Research Ethics Committee (IRB 25532-4-01).

对法学硕士生成内容的评估。The evaluation of the LLM-generated content.为了严格评估 CoT 和最终治疗类别的合理性，我们召集了三名资深肝胆专家，制定了一份符合临床实践的共识调查问卷（补充部分 A）。然后，这三位资深专家组装了一个由 60 个 HCC 病例组成的回顾性测试集，这些病例是手动策划而不是随机抽样，涵盖 (i) 从早期到晚期疾病的 BCLC、CNLC 和 AJCC/TNM 阶段，(ii) 一系列临床困难，包括边缘和充满禁忌的情况，以及 (iii) 主要候选治疗类别（切除、消融、移植转诊、TACE、全身治疗、放射治疗、支持或姑息治疗）。扩展数据图 1 总结了所得队列的基线特征。To rigorously evaluate both the CoT and the plausibility of the final treatment category, we convened three senior hepatobiliary specialists to develop a consensus-based questionnaire aligned with clinical practice (Supplementary Section A). The same three senior specialists then assembled a retrospective test set of 60 HCC cases, manually curated rather than randomly sampled, to span (i) BCLC, CNLC, and AJCC/TNM stages from early to advanced disease, (ii) a range of clinical difficulty including borderline and contraindication-laden profiles, and (iii) the major candidate-therapy categories (resection, ablation, transplant referral, TACE, systemic therapy, radiotherapy, supportive or palliative care). Baseline characteristics of the resulting cohort are summarized in Extended Data Fig. 1.

然后，我们收集了 60 个 HCC 病例的回顾性测试集，涵盖不同难度级别和候选疗法。三个模型（我们的系统、DeepSeek-v3 和 GPT-4o）的输出被随机混合并呈现给医生进行盲法评分，所有病例详细信息和模型身份均被取消识别。每个病例均由三名专家（每人至少具有15年临床经验）独立提供一线治疗的参考，并以多数决定（三分之二）作为参考标准。然后，三位资深医生使用设计的问卷对匿名 CoT 和治疗建议进行审查和评分，该问卷评估We then assembled a retrospective test set of 60 HCC cases spanning different levels of difficulty and candidate therapies. The outputs of three models (our system, DeepSeek-v3, and GPT-4o) are randomly mixed and presented to physicians for blinded scoring, with all case details and model identities de-identified. For each case, three specialists (each with at least 15 years of clinical experience) independently provide a reference for the first-line ranked treatment, and the majority decision (two out of three) serves as the reference standard. Then, three senior physicians reviewed and scored the anonymized CoT and treatment recommendations using the designed questionnaire, which evaluates完整性completeness（见补充表8），(see Supplementary Table 8),正确性correctness, 和, and安全safety（见补充表9）。综合质量得分 (QNS) 计算为这三个领域的总和。此外，为了评估循证论证的合理性，三位专家还从三个维度评估了每种推荐治疗的支持证据：(see Supplementary Table 9). The composite quality score (QNS) is calculated as the sum of these three domains. In addition, to evaluate the soundness of the evidence-based justification, three specialists also assessed the supporting evidence for each recommended treatment across three dimensions:完整性completeness , 正确性correctness和and一致性consistency .

我们的模型和医生之间的治疗准确性比较。Comparison of treatment accuracy between our model and physicians.使用相同的盲法病例组（60 个异质性 HCC 病例），我们将我们的模型与初级和中级肝胆科医生以及两名法学硕士进行了比较Using the same blinded case set (60 heterogeneous HCC cases), we compared our model with junior and intermediate hepatobiliary physicians and two LLMs on Top-𝑘治疗准确性。每位读者根据来自 EMR 的去识别化临床片段制定治疗建议，其中包括人口统计数据、主诉、现病史、既往治疗、体检结果、实验室数据和影像报告。模型身份被隐藏，病例顺序被随机化。参考标准由三名资深肝胆肿瘤学家组成的专家共识小组制定，他们独立提出符合指南的治疗方案，并按适合性评分进行排名，然后通过裁决解决任何分歧。由此产生的共识列表成为 Top- 的基本事实treatment accuracy. Each reader formulated treatment recommendations from de-identified EMR-derived clinical vignettes that included demographics, chief complaint, history of present illness, prior therapies, physical examination findings, laboratory data, and imaging reports. Model identities were concealed and case order was randomized. A reference standard was established by an expert consensus panel of three senior hepatobiliary oncologists, who independently proposed guideline-consistent treatment sets with ranked suitability scores and then resolved any disagreement through adjudication. The resulting consensus list served as the ground truth for Top-𝑘精度测量。我们还记录了任何选项是否违反绝对或实际禁忌症，这有助于结果中报告的安全通过指标。accuracy measuring. We also recorded whether any option violated absolute or practical contraindications, which contributed to the safety-pass metric reported in the Results.

通过我们基于法学硕士的系统辅助治疗决策。Assisted treatment decision-making with our LLM-based system.我们进行了一项研究，以检验我们的系统在治疗计划期间协助医生的潜在作用。在没有帮助的情况下进行初次会议后，每位初级和中级医生完成了第二次会议，其中模型的输出与相同的临床插图一起显示，包括排名的治疗方案、简明的推理原理以及标记的中间决定因素，例如We conducted a study to examine the potential role of our system in assisting physicians during treatment planning. After the initial session without assistance, each junior and intermediate physician completed a second session in which the model’s output was displayed alongside the same clinical vignette, including the ranked treatment options, concise reasoning rationales, and the tagged intermediate determinants such as<ps> , <child_pugh>, 和, and<metastasis>。辅助会议安排在基线会议后至少两周，以减少回忆。在此会议期间，医生可以自由接受、重新排序或推翻模型的建议，然后提交最终的排名列表。我们比较了顶部-. The assisted session was scheduled at least two weeks after the baseline session to mitigate recall. During this session, physicians were free to accept, reorder, or override the model’s suggestions and then submit a final ranked list. We compared Top-𝑘评估辅助前后的准确性和决策时间，并将辅助性能与独立模型和高级专家共识进行基准比较，这使我们能够评估将法学硕士整合到工作流程中是否可以提高初级和中级医生的决策质量。accuracy and decision time before and after assistance and benchmarked the assisted performance against the standalone model and the senior-expert consensus, which allowed us to assess whether integrating the LLM into the workflow improved decision quality for junior and intermediate physicians.

5 统计分析5 Statistical analysis

除非另有说明，我们报告通过患者水平引导估计的两侧 95% 置信区间 (CI)。我们的模型和基线之间的成对 C 指数比较使用双边测试We report two-sided 95% confidence intervals (CIs) estimated by patient-level bootstrap unless stated otherwise. Pairwise C-index comparisons between our model and baselines use two-sided tests with𝑃以数字和表格报告的值。时间相关的 ROC 比较在适当时使用 DeLong 的测试或基于 IPCW 的变体。对于 Kaplan-Meier 分析，将组与双边对数秩检验进行比较。中位生存率和 95% CI 的报告使用values reported in figures and tables. Time-dependent ROC comparisons use DeLong’s test or IPCW-based variants when appropriate. For Kaplan–Meier analyses, groups are compared with the two-sided log-rank test. Median survival and 95% CIs are reported using

20

标准 KM 估计量（基于 Greenwood 的误差；适用时中位数的 Brookmeyer-Crowley CI）。风险比 (HR) 和 95% CI 由比例风险模型近似得出。在临床研究中，两侧standard KM estimators (Greenwood-based errors; Brookmeyer–Crowley CIs for medians when applicable). Hazard ratios (HRs) and 95% CIs are approximated from proportional-hazards models. In clinical studies, a two-sided𝑃小于 0.05 的值被认为具有统计显着性。value of less than 0.05 is considered statistically significant.

6 数据可用性6 Data availability

本研究中使用的监测、流行病学和最终结果 (SEER) 计划数据可从国家癌症研究所公开获取。研究人员可以根据 SEER 数据使用协议和引文指南，通过 SEER 网站和 SEER*Stat 门户请求访问和下载 SEER 文件（The Surveillance, Epidemiology, and End Results (SEER) program data used in this study are publicly available from the National Cancer Institute. Researchers can request access and download SEER files via the SEER website and SEER*Stat portal, in accordance with the SEER data-use agreement and citation guidance (https://seer.cancer.gov/）。本次工作收集的多中心电子病历 (EMR) 包含来自中国 12 家三级医院的去识别化但敏感的真实临床数据。根据机构审查委员会的批准和当地有关患者隐私的法规，这些数据不能存放在公共存储库中。根据责任作者的合理要求，可以将去识别化的 EMR 用于非商业、学术研究（). The multi-center electronic medical records (EMRs) assembled for this work comprise de-identified but sensitive real-world clinical data from 12 tertiary hospitals across China. In compliance with institutional review board approvals and local regulations on patient privacy, these data cannot be deposited in a public repository. De-identified EMRs can be made available for non-commercial, academic research upon reasonable request to the responsible authors (cui-peng@mail.tsinghua.edu.cn和anddongjiahong@mail.tsinghua.edu.cn），须经参与机构批准。请求应包括简短的研究计划、请求者机构的道德批准（或豁免）证明，以及签署的数据使用协议，指定安全处理、不重新识别和不重新分发。请求通常会在 20 个工作日内得到审核；如果获得批准，将根据协议条款授予有时间限制的读取访问权限（例如 12 个月）。), subject to approval by the participating institutions. Requests should include a brief research proposal, proof of ethics approval (or exemption) from the requester’s institution, and a signed data-use agreement specifying secure handling, no re-identification, and no redistribution. Requests are typically reviewed within 20 working days; if approved, time-limited read access (e.g., 12 months) will be granted under the terms of the agreement.

图表中的汇总数据（例如，C 指数、AUROC 值和 Kaplan-Meier 估计）作为本文的源数据提供。Aggregate data underlying the figures and tables (for example, C-index, AUROC values, and Kaplan–Meier estimates) are provided as Source Data with this paper.

7 代码可用性7 Code availability

所有深度学习实验都是使用 Python (3.12) 和 PyTorch (2.7.0) 开发的。我们使用了以下标准库：NumPy 2.1.2、pandas 2.3.1、SciPy (1.16.1)、scikit-learn (1.2.1)、Transformers (4.53.0)、vLLM (0.9.2) 和 Matplotlib (3.10.5)。 Transformers 提供分词器/模型接口，scikit-learn 和 SciPy 用于度量计算和统计测试，pandas 和 NumPy 处理数据预处理，Matplotlib 用于图形生成； vLLM 用于高效推理和服务。在 PyTorch (2.7.0) 和 Verl (0.5.0) 上实现了监督微调 (SFT) 和强化学习。具体来说，我们在 SFT 初始化的主干基础上实施了知识对齐和经验累积推理，以优化治疗推荐和生存预测的复合奖励。这些代码可在 GitHub 上用于科学研究和非商业用途：All deep learning experiments were developed in Python (3.12) with PyTorch (2.7.0). We used the following standard libraries: NumPy 2.1.2, pandas 2.3.1, SciPy (1.16.1), scikit-learn (1.2.1), Transformers (4.53.0), vLLM (0.9.2), and Matplotlib (3.10.5). Transformers provided tokenizer/model interfaces, scikit-learn and SciPy were used for metric computation and statistical testing, pandas and NumPy handled data preprocessing, and Matplotlib was used for figure generation; vLLM was used for efficient inference and serving. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning were implemented on PyTorch (2.7.0) and Verl (0.5.0). Specifically, we implemented Knowledge-Aligned and Experience-Accrual reasoning to optimize the composite reward for treatment recommendation and survival prediction, building on the SFT-initialized backbone. The codes are available for scientific research and non-commercial use on GitHub athttps://github.com/Aries-iai/HCC-STAR .

扩展数据图 4：LLM 主干的治疗准确性和一致性指数（C 指数）的监督微调 (SFT) 性能。甲、乙Extended Data Fig. 4: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) performance for treatment accuracy and Concordance index (C-index) across LLM backbones. a, b, 顶-, Top-𝑘内部（a）和外部（b）测试队列的治疗建议准确性。每个模型都会输出候选疗法的排名列表和准确性 -treatment recommendation accuracy on the internal (a) and external (b) test cohorts. Each model outputs a ranked list of candidate therapies, and accuracy-𝑘当参考治疗出现在顶部时，计算出正确的预测counts a correct prediction when the reference treatment appears within the top-𝑘职位（positions (𝑘 = 1 , 2 , 3). 丙、丁c, d，内部 (c) 和外部 (d) 队列的总体、1 年、3 年和 5 年生存预测的 C 指数。跨指标和队列，, C-index for overall, 1-year, 3-year, and 5-year survival prediction on the internal (c) and external (d) cohorts. Across metrics and cohorts,Qwen3-32B在所有评估的骨干网中始终实现最高的 SFT 性能，激励其用作我们整个训练流程中后续强化学习 (RL) 优化的基础。consistently achieves the highest SFT performance among all evaluated backbones, motivating its use as the foundation for subsequent reinforcement-learning (RL) optimization in our full training pipeline.

Harrell’s C-index for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and SFT+RL on the internal SEER test set and the external multi-center cohort, evaluated overall and at 1/3/5 years. Dark segments denote the incremental gain of C-index from RL. RL yielded especially pronounced gains on the external test set, demonstrating strong generalization.

附录 A 客观医师评分问卷 (CoT)Appendix A Objective Physician Scoring Questionnaire (CoT)

范围。Scope.这个基于共识的调查问卷由高级肝胆专家开发，评估人工智能生成的内容This consensus-based questionnaire, developed by senior hepatobiliary specialists, evaluates AI-generated content at the治疗treatment水平（切除、消融、移植转诊、TACE、放射治疗、全身治疗、临床试验、支持/姑息治疗、推迟和重新评估）。它不评估特定药物或剂量。level (resection, ablation, transplant referral, TACE, radiotherapy, systemic therapy, clinical trial, supportive/palliative care, defer-and-reassess). It does not assess specific drugs or dosing.

A.3 正确性（0-25；演绎规则）A.3 Correctness (0–25; deduction rule)

错误计数（每项减去）：Count of errors (subtract per item):主要（每项-2）： □ 0 □ 1 □ 2 □ ≥3 次要（每项-1）： □ 0 □ 1 □ 2 □ ≥3Major (each −2): □ 0 □ 1 □ 2 □ ≥3 Minor (each −1): □ 0 □ 1 □ 2 □ ≥3

主要错误示例：Examples of major errors:

  • 临床分期分配不正确 (BCLC/CNLC/TNM)。Incorrect clinical staging assignment (BCLC/CNLC/TNM).

小错误示例：Examples of minor errors:

  1. 推荐选项与适合阶段的指南不一致（例如，特定阶段的 CNLC 建议）。Recommended option inconsistent with stage-appropriate guideline (e.g., stage-specific CNLC recommendations).

  2. 考虑到阶段和资格，“最佳选择”的选择是不合适的。“Best option” selection is inappropriate given the stage and eligibility.

  3. 建议表面上与指南一致，但违反了该选项的激活条件或禁忌症。Recommendation appears guideline-concordant on the surface but violates activating conditions or contraindications for that option.

最终正确率分数Final correctness score= 25 − 2 ×（主要）− 1 ×（次要）：= 25 − 2 × (major) − 1 × (minor):

A.4 安全和危险信号A.4 Safety and Red Flags

有红旗出现吗？ （任何“是”Any red flag present? (any “Yes” ⇒ 安全-失败）：Safety-Fail):□ 是（□ Yes (<2 个危险信号） □ 否（2 red flags) □ No (>= 2 个危险信号）= 2 red flags)安全评分：Safety score:25 - 5 ×（红旗）25 - 5 × (red flag)

A.5 总结和采纳决定A.5 Summary and Adoption Decision

评分笔记Scoring Notes

  1. 问卷评估The questionnaire assesses治疗类别treatment-category仅提供建议；它不会对特定药物或剂量进行评分。recommendations only; it does not score specific drugs or dosing.

  2. 完整性是根据所涵盖的所需项目的数量来映射的。Completeness is mapped from the count of required items covered.

  3. 正确性采用扣除规则：从25开始；每个主要错误减去 2，每个次要错误减去 1；楼层为 0。Correctness uses a deduction rule: start at 25; subtract 2 per major error and 1 per minor error; floor at 0.

  4. 安全使用红旗机制：任何红旗⇒安全失败且红旗数量少于2个⇒安全通过。Safety uses a red-flag mechanism: any red flag ⇒ Safety-Fail and the number of red flags is less than 2 ⇒ Safety-Pass.

  5. 综合和采用阈值：QNS ≥ 80 和安全通行证 ⇒Composite and adoption thresholds: QNS ≥ 80 and Safety-Pass ⇒采用Adopt; 70≤QNS; 70 ≤ QNS<80 和安全通行证 ⇒80 and Safety-Pass ⇒修改后采纳Adopt with edits;否则; otherwise不采纳Do not adopt .

附录 B 循证论证的评分标准Appendix B Scoring rubric for evidence-based justification

请按照以下三个基于证据的维度对三个决策过程中的每一个进行评分。每个维度满分为 10 分。Please rate each of the three decision processes along the following three evidence-based dimensions. Each dimension is scored out of 10 points.

  1. 引用证据的完整性Completeness of cited evidence（是否每个推荐选项都附有证据引用）。(whether every recommended option is accompanied by an evidence citation).10分10 points：每个推荐选项都附有证据引用；: every recommended option has an accompanying evidence citation;5分5 points：大多数选项都有证据；: most options have evidence;3分3 points：只有少数选项有证据；: only a minority of options have evidence;0分0 points: 没有任何选项有证据。: no options have evidence.

  2. 与指标患者的相关性Relevance to the index patient（引用的证据是否正确对应当前患者的病情）。(whether the cited evidence correctly corresponds to the current patient’s condition).10分10 points：所有引用的证据与当前患者高度相关；: all cited evidence is highly relevant to the current patient;5分5 points：引用最多的证据是相关的；: most cited evidence is relevant;3分3 points：只有少数是相关的；: only a minority is relevant;0分0 points：所引用的证据均不相关。: none of the cited evidence is relevant.

  3. 指南引用的准确性Accuracy of guideline citation（是否正确引用了指南来源）。(whether guideline sources are cited correctly).

  4. 10分10 points：所有指南引用均正确；: all guideline citations are correct;5分5 points：大部分是正确的；: most are correct;3分3 points：只有少数是正确的；: only a minority are correct;0分0 points: 没有一个是正确的。: none are correct.

  5. 示例：Example:如果声明内容为“对于直径≤1cm的肝细胞癌，手术切除的总生存率与消融相似或略好（证据级别3，推荐B）”，但指南中不存在所引用的证据级别，则视为错误引用。if a statement reads “For hepatocellular carcinoma with diameter ≤ 1 cm, overall survival with surgical resection is similar to or slightly better than ablation (evidence level 3, recommendation B),” but the cited evidence level does not exist in the guideline, this is counted as an incorrect citation.

清单 3：EMR 生成提示Listing 3: EMR-generation prompt

~~�~~ � # 说明 假设您是一名高级临床医学研究员，您现在需要完成以下任务： 您将获得患者的相关信息，包括：� # Instruction Assume that you are a senior clinical medical researcher, and you now need to complete the following tasks: You will be given relevant information about a patient, including:

1. 患者的一些基本信息1. Some basic information about the patient

2. 患者治疗前的一些实验室检查结果2. Some laboratory test results of the patient before treatment

# 说明# Instruction
想象一下，您是一名高级肝胆外科医生。给定患者基线、实验室和影像，产生内部“思考”Imagine you are a senior hepatobiliary surgeon. Given the patient baseline, labs, and imaging, produce the internal "thinking"
在最终建议之前。对每个选项 (0−1) 进行评分并证明其合理性。before the fnal recommendation. Rate each option (0−1) and justify.
# 输入（可能包括）# Inputs (may include)
−基本信息、合并症、肝炎、既往治疗、...−Basic info, comorbidities, hepatitis, prior therapy, ...
−实验室测试（ALB、TBIL、INR、AFP...）、Child−Pugh−Laboratory tests (ALB, TBIL, INR, AFP, ...), Child−Pugh
−影像报告（肿瘤数量/大小、节段、VI、EHM，...）−Imaging reports (tumor number/size, segment, VI, EHM, ...)
为您提供的治疗方案包括：The treatment options you are presented with include:
1）手术切除1) Surgical resection
2) 消融2) Ablation
...
# 任务：除了为每个治疗选项分配推荐分数外，您还需要提供您的逐步指导# Tasks: In addition to assigning a recommendation score to each treatment option, you are required to provide your step−by−
步骤推理。step reasoning.
1) 从输入中提取关键证据（PS、Child−Pugh、肿瘤数量/大小、VI、EHM、...）。1) Extract key evidence from input (PS, Child−Pugh, tumor number/size, VI, EHM, ...).
2）根据之前提供的决策树在CNLC/BCLC/TNM中定位分支（...）。2) Locate the branch in CNLC/BCLC/TNM according to the previously provided decision trees (...).
3) 从 CNLC 分支，列出本阶段所有指南建议的替代方案 (...)。3) From the CNLC branch, list all guideline−suggested alternatives for this stage (...).
4) 对于每个替代方案，评估：满足适应症、未违反禁忌症、缺失但需要的信息、合理的推断（如果）4) For each alternative, assess: indications met, contraindications not violated, missing−but−needed info, reasonable inference if
任意、证据级别和推荐级别。any, evidence level and recommendation grade.
5）比较备选方案并排名；给出 0−1 分，并给出简短的理由。5) Compare alternatives and rank; assign scores 0−1 with short reasons.
# 检查规则# Checking rules
−“CNLC 的初步决定”必须包括 CNLC 阶段建议的所有选项。−The "preliminary decision by CNLC" must include all options suggested by that CNLC stage.
−“详细分析”必须涵盖上面列出的每个选项。−The "detailed analysis" must cover every option listed above.
−CNLC、BCLC、TNM 测定必须与之前显示的决策树一致。−CNLC, BCLC, TNM determinations must be consistent with the decision trees shown earlier.
# 背景知识# Background knowledge
−BCLC 树摘要：...−BCLC tree summary: ...
−CNLC 树摘要：...−CNLC tree summary: ...
